You are reviewing the work of a data agent that has just finished a task. The agent wrote code, ran it, and looked at results. You will see its complete trajectory, together with the task, the specification and documentation it used, and an index of all available data.

Your job is to find the places where the agent's work may be wrong, and to rank them by importance. Do not solve the task, do not write code, and do not judge whether the agent's final answer is correct as a whole.

Look for two kinds of problems:
- Places where the agent's work does not match what the task, specification, or documentation requires: scope and filters, metric definitions, which column a business term refers to, handling of special cases (missing values, zero denominators, invalid values), and the required form, granularity, and type of the output.
- Places where the agent's work may rest on a wrong understanding of the data: what a field or value means, what a row represents, whether a key is unique, whether records match in a join, what nulls mean, units, formats, where counting starts, and the time range or entities the data covers.

Listing a possible problem is not a claim that it is a real error. Many will turn out to be fine; they are listed so that they can be checked later. A possible problem that is unlikely but would change the result a lot if it were real should still be listed, ranked lower.

Two kinds of problems are easy to miss, so check for them explicitly:
- Omitted steps: processing that the specification or documentation requires but the code does not perform.
- Unused materials: files, tables, or columns in the index that bear on the task but that the agent did not use.

Rank problems by importance, combining two things: how likely it is to be a real problem given the materials you can see, and how much the agent's result would change if it is. A possible problem that would change little should rank low.

Return a JSON object whose keys are the names of every table or result the agent produced. For each, give an object with:
- "items": an ordered list of the 10 most important possible problems, most important first. Each item has:
  - "problem": one sentence describing what may be wrong.
  - "about": "requirement" or "data".
  - "evidence_in_work": a short quote from the agent's code or output that shows where the problem is.
  - "source": a short quote from the task, specification, documentation, or index that the problem is based on, or null.
  - "importance": an integer from 1 (low) to 5 (high).
  - "reason": one short sentence explaining the importance.
- "fewer_reason": if you listed fewer than 10 items, explain why; otherwise null.
List fewer than 10 only if you cannot find 10 possible problems for that table or result.

Examples of possible problems (from an unrelated online-shop task; they show the form of an item, not what to look for):
- {"problem": "If the amount column in payments is in cents rather than US dollars, revenue would be off by a factor of 100.", "about": "data", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(p.amount) AS revenue_usd", "source": null, "importance": 2, "reason": "The column name suggests dollars, so this is unlikely, but the effect would be large."}
- {"problem": "Revenue is computed from total_charged, which may include shipping fees, while the specification may define revenue as item price times quantity.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(o.total_charged) AS revenue_usd", "source": "revenue: sum of item_price * quantity", "importance": 4, "reason": "If the specification's definition applies, every row of revenue would be too high."}
- {"problem": "The specification requires converting created_at to UTC before deriving order_date, but the code takes the date directly.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "CAST(o.created_at AS DATE) AS order_date", "source": "convert created_at to UTC before deriving order_date", "importance": 5, "reason": "Orders near midnight would be assigned to the wrong day."}

=== Task ===
## Task Description:
You are a professional Data Engineer responsible for implementing a complete SQL data pipeline based on data contract documents.

## Objectives
Populate the SQL files in the `./sql/` directory according to the specifications in `docs/data_contract.yaml` and `config/layer_dependencies.yaml`. Your task is clear: write the necessary SQL code to ensure `run.py` executes successfully.

You MUST carefully review the content of `docs/data_contract.yaml`. You may use tools to view it. Since the file content is long, you can view it in chunks. Your task is to complete the SQL file writing based on the content of this file.

## Project Background
* Raw data has been loaded into `xxxx_start.duckdb` (Note: 'xxxx' is a placeholder for the project name, such as `greenhouse_start.duckdb` or `google_ads_start.duckdb`; 'xxxx' appearing below carries the same meaning).
* Currently, running `python run.py` completes the data transformation and outputs `xxxx.duckdb`.
* The existing SQL project is stored in the `sql/` folder.
* The current requirements documentation is located in `docs/data_contract.yaml`.

**Implementation Requirements:**
1. **Strictly Adhere to Configuration**:
    * All SQL logic and dependencies must be written strictly according to the `staging`, `intermediate`, and `marts` hierarchy defined in the YAML configuration files.

2. **File and Directory Structure**:
    * SQL files must be created in the corresponding level subdirectories (e.g., `sql/staging/`, `sql/intermediate/`, `sql/marts/`).
    * Strictly follow predefined file naming conventions and coding styles.

3. **Use Pure DuckDB Syntax**:
    * All SQL code must be compatible with DuckDB.
    * **Do NOT** end SQL statements with a semicolon (`;`) at the end of the file.

4. **No dbt Syntax**:
    * You must reference upstream tables directly using the `schema.table_name` format.
    * **Do NOT** use the dbt `ref()` function.

    **Examples**:
    * **Correct ✅**:
```sql
    with account_base as (
    select *
    from staging.stg_salesforce__account
    where not is_deleted
    ),

    account_enriched as (
    select
    account_id,
    ...
```

* **Incorrect ❌**:
```sql
    WITH accounts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__account') }}}}
    ),
    contacts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__contact') }}}}
    ),
    ...
```
Output Standard: Ensure the generated SQL files can be successfully executed by run.py to produce high-quality data results that meet business requirements.

```yaml
version: "1.0"
metadata:
  description: "Complete data pipeline specification for Salesforce CRM data transformation and analytics"
  author: "Data Engineering Team"
  pipeline_phases: ["staging_contract", "modeling_spec"]
  created_date: "2024-09-11"
  source_system: "Salesforce CRM"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# PHASE 1: Defines the transformation from raw data to clean staging tables.  #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
staging_contract:
  purpose: "Transform raw Salesforce data into clean, validated, and standardized staging tables with comprehensive data quality controls and business rule enforcement"

  quality_actions:
    correct:
      description: "Automatically fix deterministic format issues such as email casing, whitespace trimming, and standardized date formats"
      examples: ["LOWER(TRIM(email))", "standardize timezone formats", "normalize phone numbers"]
    
    delete_row:
      description: "Remove entire row when records contain severe data quality issues that make them unusable downstream."
      examples: ["Explicitly marked for deletion", "Confirmed duplicates", "Invalid primary keys", "Essential business attributes contain irrecoverable errors"]
    
    nullify_field:
      description: "Set invalid field values to NULL when the field is non-critical and record can still be useful"
      examples: ["malformed optional URLs", "invalid secondary contact information", "corrupted metadata fields"]

  tables:
[Per-table sections of the data contract are omitted here. The sections relevant to each review appear under "Relevant specification and documentation".]
modeling_spec:
  purpose: "Transform staging data into business-ready analytical models that support sales performance analysis, pipeline management, and revenue forecasting"

  design_principles:
    intermediate_purpose: "Create reusable, business-centric data models that implement core business logic and serve as building blocks for multiple analytical use cases"
    marts_purpose: "Deliver analysis-ready models optimized for specific business questions and reporting requirements"
    grain_importance: "Every model must have a clearly defined grain that specifies what each row represents, ensuring consistent interpretation and preventing aggregation errors"

  intermediate_models:
  marts_models:

```

## stg_salesforce__account

    - name: stg_salesforce__account
      description: "Cleaned and standardized company/organization records from Salesforce with validated business information"
      source_table: raw.account
      columns:
        - name: account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "account_id IS NOT NULL"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "account_id ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9]{15,18}$'"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "account_id IN (SELECT id FROM raw.account)"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: account_number
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: account_name
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "account_name IS NOT NULL"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "TRIM(account_name) != ''"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "account_name = TRIM(account_name)"
              on_failure: correct
        - name: account_description
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: account_source
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: industry
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: annual_revenue
          data_type: DECIMAL(15,2)
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "annual_revenue IS NULL OR annual_revenue >= 0"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "annual_revenue IS NULL OR annual_revenue <= 999999999999.99"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: number_of_employees
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: website
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: owner_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: type
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: billing_city
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: billing_country
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: billing_postal_code
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: billing_state
          data_type: VARCHAR

          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: billing_street
          data_type: VARCHAR

          data_type: DATE
        - name: last_referenced_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
        - name: last_viewed_date

# PHASE 2: Defines the business logic from staging tables to analytical models. #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


## int_salesforce__account_enhanced

    - name: int_salesforce__account_enhanced
      description: Enhanced Salesforce account records with derived size, region, age, maturity, completeness, and high-value flags; filtered to non-deleted accounts
      grain: One row per account_id (non-deleted)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_salesforce__account and keep only rows where is_deleted is false. - Pass through source fields and rename: type → account_type; industry → industry_normalized. - Derive account_size_segment from annual_revenue: - >= 10,000,000 → "Enterprise"; >= 1,000,000 → "Mid-Market"; >= 100,000 → "SMB"; > 0 → "Small"; otherwise "Unknown" (includes NULL and <= 0). - Derive geographic_region from billing_country after uppercasing for comparison: - {"UNITED STATES","US","USA","CANADA"} → "North America"; {"UNITED KINGDOM","UK","GERMANY","FRANCE"} → "Europe"; all others (including NULL) → "Other". - Derive account_age_days: - If last_activity_date is not NULL, compute the number of days from last_activity_date (date only) to today; else NULL. Negative values are possible if last_activity_date is in the future. - Derive account_maturity_stage: - If last_activity_date is NULL → "Unknown"; else if account_age_days < 365 → "Active"; else → "Mature". - Derive data_completeness_score: - Add 50 points if website is present and not empty after trimming whitespace; add 50 points if annual_revenue is present and > 0; possible values are 0, 50, or 100. - Derive is_high_value_account: - TRUE if annual_revenue >= 5,000,000; otherwise FALSE (NULL treated as FALSE). - Include is_deleted from the filtered set; it will always be FALSE.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Primary key from source; exactly one output row per account_id in non-deleted accounts
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name passed through from source unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: account_number
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account number passed through from source unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: account_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Direct rename of source column type; values passed through unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: account_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account source passed through from source unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: industry_normalized
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Direct rename of source column industry; no additional normalization; NULLs preserved
      - name: account_size_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Revenue-based segment: >=10,000,000 Enterprise; >=1,000,000 Mid-Market; >=100,000 SMB; >0 Small; else Unknown; computed from annual_revenue with NULL and <=0 → Unknown'
      - name: geographic_region
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Region based on uppercased billing_country: North America if in {UNITED STATES, US, USA, CANADA}; Europe if in {UNITED KINGDOM, UK, GERMANY, FRANCE}; else Other; NULL → Other'
      - name: account_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days from last_activity_date (date) to today; NULL if last_activity_date is NULL; negative values possible if last_activity_date is in the future
      - name: account_maturity_stage
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unknown if last_activity_date is NULL; else Active if account_age_days < 365; else Mature
      - name: data_completeness_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: '0, 50, or 100: +50 if website is present and not empty after trimming; +50 if annual_revenue is present and > 0; never NULL'
      - name: is_high_value_account
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: TRUE if annual_revenue >= 5,000,000; else FALSE; NULL annual_revenue treated as FALSE; never NULL
      - name: annual_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(15,2)
        description: Annual revenue passed through from source; scale 2; NULLs and negatives preserved
      - name: number_of_employees
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Employee count passed through from source unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: billing_street
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing street from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: billing_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing city from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: billing_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing state from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: billing_postal_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing postal code from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: billing_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing country from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved (classification uses uppercase internally)
      - name: website
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Website passed through unchanged; completeness scoring treats it as non-empty only if present and not just whitespace; NULLs preserved
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner ID passed through from source unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: last_activity_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Last activity date passed through from source; used in age/maturity derivations; NULLs preserved
      - name: last_referenced_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Last referenced timestamp from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: last_viewed_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Last viewed timestamp from source; unchanged; NULLs preserved
      - name: is_deleted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Deletion flag from source; always FALSE in output due to pre-filter; never NULL


## int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline

    - name: int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline
      description: Opportunity pipeline with account enrichment, stage scoring, size categorization, weighted value, and date-based risk/status metrics
      grain: One row per opportunity_id; may duplicate if multiple account_enhanced matches per account_id; only non-deleted opportunities
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity filtered to is_deleted = false. - Left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced on account_id; if multiple matches exist, rows duplicate; if no match, account fields are NULL. - Pass through opportunity fields: opportunity_id, opportunity_name, account_id, stage_name, amount, expected_revenue, probability, forecast_category, lead_source, campaign_id, fiscal_quarter, fiscal_year, is_closed, is_won, close_date, owner_id, created_date, is_deleted. - Bring account fields: account_name, account_size_segment, is_high_value_account, geographic_region, industry_normalized. - stage_progression_score: exact string mapping on stage_name → Prospecting:10; Qualification:20; Needs Analysis:30; Value Proposition:40; Proposal/Price Quote:70; Closed Won:100; Closed Lost:0; all others:50. - deal_size_category: by amount with inclusive thresholds and strict positivity → ≥100000:Large; ≥25000:Medium; ≥5000:Small; >0:Micro; else:Unknown. NULL, 0, or negative amounts map to Unknown. - weighted_pipeline_value: treat NULL amount and probability as 0; compute (amount × probability) ÷ 100; floating-point result; no rounding. - win_probability_confidence: from probability (percent) → ≥75:High Confidence; ≥50:Medium Confidence; ≥25:Low Confidence; else:Very Low. NULL probability falls to Very Low. - opportunity_age_days: number of days from the date part of created_date to today; time-varying; NULL if created_date is NULL; can be negative if created_date is in the future. - deal_velocity_risk: based on opportunity_age_days → >180:High Risk; >90:Medium Risk; else:Low Risk. If age is NULL (created_date missing), result is Low Risk. - close_date_status: if close_date is before today:Overdue; if on or before 90 days from today:Current Quarter; else:On Track. NULL close_date is treated as On Track. - days_to_close: number of days from today to the date part of close_date; NULL if close_date is NULL; negative if in the past, 0 if today. - is_strategic_deal: true if amount ≥ 500000 or account_size_segment equals ''Enterprise''; else false. NULL inputs evaluate to false. - is_deleted output remains false due to the initial filter.'
      columns:
      - name: opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique opportunity identifier; duplicates may occur if the account join returns multiple matches
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: opportunity_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity name from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account ID owning the opportunity from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from int_salesforce__account_enhanced via left join; NULL if no account match
      - name: account_size_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account size segment from int_salesforce__account_enhanced; used by is_strategic_deal (exact string match)
      - name: is_high_value_account
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: High-value account flag from int_salesforce__account_enhanced; may be NULL if no account match
      - name: geographic_region
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Geographic region from int_salesforce__account_enhanced; NULL if no account match
      - name: industry_normalized
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Normalized industry from int_salesforce__account_enhanced; NULL if no account match
      - name: stage_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Sales stage name from stg_salesforce__opportunity; exact string used for stage scoring
      - name: stage_progression_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 'Stage score mapped from stage_name: Prospecting=10, Qualification=20, Needs Analysis=30, Value Proposition=40, Proposal/Price Quote=70, Closed Won=100, Closed Lost=0, else=50'
      - name: amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Opportunity amount; used for size categorization, weighted value, and strategic flag; may be NULL
      - name: deal_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Deal size by amount: ≥100000 Large; ≥25000 Medium; ≥5000 Small; >0 Micro; else Unknown (NULL/0/negative → Unknown)'
      - name: expected_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(15,2)
        description: Expected revenue from stg_salesforce__opportunity; passthrough
      - name: probability
        data_type: DECIMAL(5,2)
        description: Win probability percent from stg_salesforce__opportunity; NULL treated as 0 for weighting and as Very Low for confidence
      - name: weighted_pipeline_value
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Computed as (amount × probability) ÷ 100 with NULLs treated as 0; floating-point result with no rounding
      - name: win_probability_confidence
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Confidence tier from probability: ≥75 High, ≥50 Medium, ≥25 Low, else Very Low; NULL probability → Very Low'
      - name: opportunity_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days from created_date (date part) to today; time-varying; NULL if created_date is NULL; can be negative if created_date is in the future
      - name: deal_velocity_risk
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Risk tier from opportunity_age_days: >180 High, >90 Medium, else Low; NULL age (missing created_date) → Low'
      - name: close_date_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Status vs today: before today Overdue; on or before 90 days from today Current Quarter; else On Track; NULL close_date → On Track'
      - name: days_to_close
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days from today to close_date (date part); NULL if close_date is NULL; negative if in the past
      - name: is_strategic_deal
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if amount ≥ 500000 or account_size_segment is 'Enterprise'; else false; NULL inputs evaluate to false
      - name: forecast_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Forecast category from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead source from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Campaign ID from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: fiscal_quarter
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal quarter number from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: fiscal_year
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal year from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: is_closed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Closed flag from stg_salesforce__opportunity; passthrough
      - name: is_won
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Won flag from stg_salesforce__opportunity; passthrough
      - name: close_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Close date from stg_salesforce__opportunity; used for status and days_to_close; may be NULL
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user ID from stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Creation timestamp from stg_salesforce__opportunity; date part used for age calculations
      - name: is_deleted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Deletion flag from stg_salesforce__opportunity; always false due to initial filter


## int_salesforce__activity_summary

    - name: int_salesforce__activity_summary
      description: Account-level summary from Salesforce tasks and events for active accounts; counts are across all filtered activities (no time windows), and several fields are fixed placeholders per current SQL.
      grain: One row per active (is_deleted=false) account_id
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__task
      - staging.stg_salesforce__event
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      business_logic: '- Define the output account set from staging.stg_salesforce__account where is_deleted=false; return one row per account_id. - Build task_activities from staging.stg_salesforce__task: - Keep rows where is_deleted=false, what_id is not null, and what_id has length 18 characters. - Set account_id from what_id, carry owner_id, set created_date from Task.created_date, and label activity_type as "Task". - Build event_activities from staging.stg_salesforce__event: - Keep rows where is_deleted=false, what_id is not null, and what_id has length 18 characters. - Set account_id from what_id, carry owner_id, set created_date from Event.activity_date, and label activity_type as "Event". - Union all task_activities and event_activities; duplicates are retained. - Aggregate per account_id (no date windowing is applied despite column names): - total_activities_30d, total_activities_90d, total_activities_365d: count of all unioned activity rows; these three values are identical. - completed_tasks_30d, open_tasks_30d, total_events_30d: constant 0. - avg_meeting_duration_minutes: constant 0.0. - unique_activity_owners: number of distinct owner_id values, ignoring nulls. - activity_frequency_score: constant "Medium". - last_activity_date: the latest activity date (date component of created_date). - days_since_last_activity: whole days between last_activity_date and the current date; null if no activity. - engagement_temperature: constant "Warm". - activity_trend_30d: constant "Stable". - is_declining_engagement: constant false. - most_active_user_id: constant 1. - activity_diversity_score: constant 50. - Left join these metrics to the active account set on account_id, and join staging.stg_salesforce__account again to pull account_name. - Apply defaults when no metrics exist for an account (i.e., no activities): - account_name: "Unknown" if null. - total counts and scores: 0 (use 0.0 for avg_meeting_duration_minutes). - activity_frequency_score: "Minimal". - engagement_temperature: "Cold". - activity_trend_30d: "New". - is_declining_engagement: false. - most_active_user_id, last_activity_date, and days_since_last_activity remain null.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce account identifier for active accounts; one output row per account_id.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from staging.stg_salesforce__account; defaults to 'Unknown' when null.
      - name: total_activities_30d
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of all filtered task and event activities for the account (no 30-day window); defaults to 0.
      - name: total_activities_90d
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of all filtered activities (no 90-day window); equals total_activities_30d; defaults to 0.
      - name: total_activities_365d
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of all filtered activities (no 365-day window); equals total_activities_30d; defaults to 0.
      - name: completed_tasks_30d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Constant 0 per current SQL; defaults to 0.
      - name: open_tasks_30d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Constant 0 per current SQL; defaults to 0.
      - name: total_events_30d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Constant 0 per current SQL; defaults to 0.
      - name: avg_meeting_duration_minutes
        data_type: DECIMAL(2,1)
        description: Constant 0.0 minutes; defaults to 0.0.
      - name: unique_activity_owners
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of owner_id across filtered activities; null owners ignored; defaults to 0.
      - name: activity_frequency_score
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Categorical score: ''Medium'' when any activity exists, else ''Minimal''.'
      - name: last_activity_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Latest activity date for the account (date component); null when no activity.
      - name: days_since_last_activity
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days between last_activity_date and current date; null when last_activity_date is null.
      - name: engagement_temperature
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Categorical status: ''Warm'' when any activity exists, else ''Cold''.'
      - name: activity_trend_30d
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Categorical trend: ''Stable'' when any activity exists, else ''New''.'
      - name: is_declining_engagement
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Always false per current SQL; defaults to false.
      - name: most_active_user_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Constant 1 when any activity exists; null when no activity.
      - name: activity_diversity_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Constant 50 when any activity exists; defaults to 0.


## int_salesforce__date_spine

    - name: int_salesforce__date_spine
      description: Date spine from the earliest opportunity created_date to current_date (inclusive) with standard calendar attributes.
      grain: One row per calendar date from earliest opportunity created_date through current_date (inclusive)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity (created_date)
      business_logic: '- Determine the start date as the earliest created_date from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity, using only the calendar date portion. - Set the end date to the current calendar date at query execution time. - If no start date exists (no source rows), produce no rows. - Generate one row per day from the start date to the end date, inclusive, stepping by one day. - For each date_day, derive: - year: calendar year. - quarter: calendar quarter (1–4). - month: calendar month number (1–12). - week: ISO week number (1–53); the ISO week-year may differ from the calendar year. - day_of_week: day of week number where 0=Sunday, …, 6=Saturday. - day_of_year: day number within the year (1–366). - Order the output ascending by date_day.'
      columns:
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar date generated for each day from the start date to current_date (inclusive); unique, not null, and sorted ascending in the output.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar year of date_day; not null.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter of date_day (1–4); not null.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar month number of date_day (1–12); not null.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: week
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: ISO week number of date_day (1–53); ISO week-year may differ from the calendar year; not null.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: day_of_week
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Day of week for date_day where 0=Sunday..6=Saturday; not null.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: day_of_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Day number within the year for date_day (1–366); not null.
        tests:
        - not_null


## int_salesforce__lead_conversion_funnel

    - name: int_salesforce__lead_conversion_funnel
      description: Lead funnel metrics for non-deleted Salesforce leads with age, quality, source/category, conversion timing/completeness, and temperature
      grain: One row per lead_id (non-deleted leads)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__lead
      business_logic: '1) Start from staging.stg_salesforce__lead and include only leads where is_deleted = false. 2) Compute lead_name by joining first_name and last_name with a single space; treat NULLs as empty strings; remove leading/trailing spaces from the result. 3) Compute lead_age_days: - If is_converted is true: number of days between the date part of created_date and the date part of converted_date. - Else: number of days between the date part of created_date and the current calendar date at query time. - Result is NULL if created_date is NULL or (in the converted branch) converted_date is NULL. 4) Compute lead_quality_score (integer 10–100) as the sum of: - +20 if email is present and contains both "@" and "."; else +0. - +15 if phone is present and not empty after removing surrounding spaces; else +0. - +5 if mobile_phone is present and not empty after removing surrounding spaces; else +0. - +10 if industry is present and not empty after removing surrounding spaces; else +0. - +15 if annual_revenue is present and greater than 0; else +0. - +10 if number_of_employees is present and greater than 0; else +0. - +20 if lead_source (case-insensitive) is one of {referral, website, web, partner referral, word of mouth}; else +10. - +5 if company is present and not empty after removing surrounding spaces and, case-insensitively, not in {test, example, sample, demo, unknown, n/a, none}; else +0. 5) Map lead_source_category (case-insensitive): - Digital: {web, website, online, social media, email, google ads, linkedin} - Referral: {referral, partner referral, word of mouth, employee referral} - Marketing: {trade show, event, conference, purchased list, advertising} - Direct: {phone inquiry, direct mail, cold call} - Other: any other value, including NULL. 6) Compute conversion_time_days: if is_converted is true and converted_date is present, number of days between the date parts of created_date and converted_date; otherwise NULL. 7) Compute has_complete_conversion: true only if is_converted is true and converted_account_id, converted_contact_id, and converted_opportunity_id are all present; else false. 8) Compute lead_temperature from lead_quality_score: ≥80 → Hot; ≥60 → Warm; ≥40 → Cool; else Cold. 9) Compute conversion_speed_category from conversion_time_days: ≤7 → Fast Track; ≤30 → Standard; ≤90 → Slow; >90 → Extended; NULL if conversion_time_days is NULL. 10) Output the listed columns; pass-through fields retain original casing and spacing.'
      columns:
      - name: lead_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce lead identifier; one output row per lead_id with is_deleted=false
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: lead_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First and last names joined by a single space; NULLs treated as empty; trimmed; empty string if both are missing
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead first name from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead last name from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: company
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Company from source; contributes +5 to quality when non-empty after trimming and not in a case-insensitive blacklist of test/example/sample/demo/unknown/n/a/none
      - name: email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Email from source; contributes +20 to quality when it contains both '@' and '.'; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: phone
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Phone from source; contributes +15 to quality when non-empty after trimming; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: mobile_phone
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Mobile phone from source; contributes +5 to quality when non-empty after trimming; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead title from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Original lead source from source; used for scoring and category mapping with case-insensitive comparison; may be NULL
      - name: lead_source_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Category mapped from lead_source (case-insensitive) to Digital, Referral, Marketing, Direct, or Other; NULL maps to Other
      - name: lead_quality_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Integer 10–100 computed from defined attribute weights in business_logic; never NULL
      - name: lead_temperature
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Bucketed from lead_quality_score: Hot (≥80), Warm (≥60), Cool (≥40), else Cold; never NULL'
      - name: lead_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days from the date part of created_date to the date part of converted_date if converted, else to the current date; NULL if created_date is NULL or converted_date is NULL when converted
      - name: status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead status from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: industry
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Industry from source; contributes +10 to quality when non-empty after trimming; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: annual_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Annual revenue from source; contributes +15 to quality when >0; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: number_of_employees
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Employee count from source; contributes +10 to quality when >0; may be NULL
      - name: is_converted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Conversion flag from source; controls age and conversion metrics; may be NULL
      - name: conversion_time_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days between the date parts of created_date and converted_date when converted_date is present and is_converted is true; otherwise NULL
      - name: conversion_speed_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Bucketed by conversion_time_days: Fast Track (≤7), Standard (≤30), Slow (≤90), Extended (>90); NULL when conversion_time_days is NULL'
      - name: has_complete_conversion
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True only if is_converted is true and all three converted IDs are present; else false
      - name: converted_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Converted timestamp from source; used in conversion time and age calculations; may be NULL
      - name: converted_account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Converted Account Id from source; used to assess conversion completeness; may be NULL
      - name: converted_contact_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Converted Contact Id from source; used to assess conversion completeness; may be NULL
      - name: converted_opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Converted Opportunity Id from source; used to assess conversion completeness; may be NULL
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead owner Id from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Lead creation timestamp from source; used by taking its date part in age and conversion calculations; may be NULL
      - name: last_modified_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Last modified timestamp from source; unchanged; may be NULL
      - name: is_deleted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Deletion flag from source; all output rows have is_deleted=false due to the initial filter


## int_salesforce__user_performance

    - name: int_salesforce__user_performance
      description: Aggregated opportunity and lead metrics for active Salesforce users; unimplemented activity/time metrics are set to zero.
      grain: One row per active user_id
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - staging.stg_salesforce__lead
      business_logic: '- User scope: include only users where is_active = true from staging.stg_salesforce__user. - Opportunity metrics: - Source: staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity filtered to records that are not deleted and have an owner_id. - Group by owner_id to compute: - total_opportunities: count of opportunities. - won_opportunities: count where the opportunity is marked won. - total_won_amount: sum of amount for won opportunities; NULL amounts do not contribute. - total_pipeline_amount: sum of amount for open opportunities (not closed); NULL amounts do not contribute. - avg_won_deal_size: average amount across won opportunities; excludes NULL amounts. - Lead metrics: - Source: staging.stg_salesforce__lead filtered to records that are not deleted and have an owner_id. - Group by owner_id to compute: - total_leads: count of leads. - converted_leads: count where the lead is converted. - Assembly: - Left join both metric sets to the active user list on user_id = owner_id. - user_role_name is the constant value ''Sales''. - If a user has no matching opportunities or leads, all related numeric measures default to 0 (or 0.00 for decimals). - Rates: - win_rate_percentage: percentage of opportunities won out of total opportunities, rounded to 2 decimals; 0 when there are no opportunities. - lead_conversion_rate_percentage: percentage of leads converted out of total leads, rounded to 2 decimals; 0 when there are no leads. - activity_completion_rate_percentage: constant 0.0 (one decimal place). - Placeholders set to 0: lost_opportunities, open_opportunities, weighted_pipeline_amount, largest_won_deal, avg_sales_cycle_days, 30/90-day opportunity and lead counters, and all activity counts. - performance_tier: High Performer if won_opportunities ≥ 10; Good Performer if ≥ 5; Developing if total_opportunities > 0; otherwise New User. - is_active is taken from the user source and is always true for included rows.'
      columns:
      - name: user_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique identifier of the active user; one row per user_id.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique

## int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner

    - name: int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner
      description: Owner-level rollup of opportunity outcomes (won, lost, pipeline) with counts, amounts, timing averages, and manager resolution prioritized by pipeline then won then lost.
      grain: One row per salesforce_user.user_id (owner)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user_role
      business_logic: '1) Build an enhanced opportunity view by joining: - Opportunity to Account on account_id. - Opportunity.owner_id to User (owner). - Owner.manager_id to User (manager). - Owner.user_role_id to User Role. Derive status: - Won when is_won is true. - Lost when is_won is false and is_closed is true. - Pipeline when is_closed is false and forecast_category is one of pipeline, forecast, or bestcase (case-insensitive). Derive helper fields: - created_amount_this_month/quarter: amount when the opportunity was created in the period, otherwise 0. - created_count_this_month/quarter: 1 when created in the period, otherwise 0. - closed_amount_this_month/quarter: amount when closed in the period, otherwise 0. - closed_count_this_month/quarter: 1 when closed in the period, otherwise 0. Notes: If amount is null when a condition is true, the derived amount is null; sums and averages ignore nulls; zeros contribute as zero. 2) Won aggregation (booking_by_owner): keep rows with status = Won and aggregate by owner and their manager. - bookings_amount_closed_this_month/quarter: sum of the respective closed amounts, rounded to the nearest whole number. - total_number_bookings: number of Won opportunities. - total_bookings_amount: sum of amount, rounded to the nearest whole number. - bookings_count_closed_this_month/quarter: sum of the 1/0 closed flags (0 if group exists but no flags). - avg_bookings_amount: average amount, rounded to the nearest whole number. - largest_booking: maximum amount. - avg_days_to_close: average days_to_close. Duplicates are preserved; ordering is irrelevant. 3) Lost aggregation (lost_by_owner): keep rows with status = Lost and aggregate by owner and their manager. - lost_amount_this_month/quarter: sum of the respective closed amounts, rounded to the nearest whole number. - total_number_lost: number of Lost opportunities. - total_lost_amount: sum of amount, rounded to the nearest whole number. - lost_count_this_month/quarter: sum of the 1/0 closed flags (0 if group exists but no flags). 4) Pipeline aggregation (pipeline_by_owner): keep rows with status = Pipeline and aggregate by owner and their manager. - pipeline_created_amount_this_month/quarter: sum of the respective created amounts, rounded to the nearest whole number. - pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_month/quarter: sum of created amount multiplied by probability, rounded to the nearest whole number. - pipeline_count_created_this_month/quarter: sum of the 1/0 created flags (0 if group exists but no flags). - total_number_pipeline: number of Pipeline opportunities. - total_pipeline_amount: sum of amount, rounded to the nearest whole number. - total_pipeline_forecast_amount: sum of amount multiplied by probability, rounded to the nearest whole number. - avg_pipeline_opp_amount: average amount, rounded to the nearest whole number. - largest_deal_in_pipeline: maximum amount. - avg_days_open: average days_since_created. Products with null operands are null and are ignored by sums/averages. 5) Final output: start from all users and left join each aggregation on owner_id = user_id. manager_id is chosen by precedence: pipeline manager, else won manager, else lost manager. If an owner has no rows in all three aggregations, manager_id and all aggregate columns are null. One row per owner.'
      columns:
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce user_id of the owner; exactly one output row per owner
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager user_id selected with precedence pipeline, then won, then lost; null when the owner has no pipeline/won/lost rows
      - name: b_manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager user_id associated to the owner's won aggregation group; null when no won opportunities
      - name: b_owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user_id associated to the won aggregation group; equals owner_id when present, else null
      - name: bookings_amount_closed_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for won deals closed this month, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no won rows
      - name: bookings_amount_closed_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for won deals closed this quarter, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no won rows
      - name: total_number_bookings
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of won opportunities for the owner; null when no won rows
      - name: total_bookings_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Total amount across won opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no won rows
      - name: bookings_count_closed_this_month
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for won deals closed this month (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no won rows
      - name: bookings_count_closed_this_quarter
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for won deals closed this quarter (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no won rows
      - name: avg_bookings_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average amount for won opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when none or all amounts are null
      - name: largest_booking
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Maximum opportunity amount among won opportunities; null when no won rows
      - name: avg_days_to_close
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days_to_close among won opportunities; null when no won rows
      - name: l_manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager user_id associated to the owner's lost aggregation group; null when no lost opportunities
      - name: l_owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user_id associated to the lost aggregation group; equals owner_id when present, else null
      - name: lost_amount_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for lost deals closed this month, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no lost rows
      - name: lost_amount_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for lost deals closed this quarter, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no lost rows
      - name: total_number_lost
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of lost opportunities for the owner; null when no lost rows
      - name: total_lost_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Total amount across lost opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no lost rows
      - name: lost_count_this_month
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for lost deals closed this month (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no lost rows
      - name: lost_count_this_quarter
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for lost deals closed this quarter (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no lost rows
      - name: p_manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager user_id associated to the owner's pipeline aggregation group; null when no pipeline opportunities
      - name: p_owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user_id associated to the pipeline aggregation group; equals owner_id when present, else null
      - name: pipeline_created_amount_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities created this month in pipeline, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: pipeline_created_amount_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities created this quarter in pipeline, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of created amounts multiplied by probability for this month in pipeline, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of created amounts multiplied by probability for this quarter in pipeline, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: pipeline_count_created_this_month
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for opportunities created this month in pipeline (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no pipeline rows
      - name: pipeline_count_created_this_quarter
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of 1/0 flags for opportunities created this quarter in pipeline (0 when group exists but no flags); null when no pipeline rows
      - name: total_number_pipeline
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of pipeline opportunities for the owner; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: total_pipeline_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Total amount across pipeline opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: total_pipeline_forecast_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Total of amount multiplied by probability across pipeline opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: avg_pipeline_opp_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average amount for pipeline opportunities, rounded to the nearest whole number; null when none or all amounts are null
      - name: largest_deal_in_pipeline
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Maximum opportunity amount among pipeline opportunities; null when no pipeline rows
      - name: avg_days_open
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days_since_created among pipeline opportunities; null when no pipeline rows


## fct_salesforce__sales_pipeline

    - name: fct_salesforce__sales_pipeline
      description: Sales pipeline fact joined with account and active owner details, enriched with date parts and derived health/status metrics
      grain: One row per opportunity_id from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline; duplicates from source are preserved
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      business_logic: '- Build user_info from staging.stg_salesforce__user where is_active = true; set owner_name by concatenating first_name and last_name with a single space, treating missing names as empty, then trim leading/trailing spaces. - From intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline (op), left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced (ae) on account_id, and left join user_info (ui) on owner_id. - Derive close_year, close_quarter, close_month as the calendar year, quarter, and month of close_date; these are NULL when close_date is NULL. - Derive created_year, created_quarter, created_month as the calendar year, quarter, and month of created_date; these are NULL when created_date is NULL. - pipeline_health_score: Healthy when stage_progression_score ≥ 70, probability ≥ 50, and deal_velocity_risk is New or Low Risk; Moderate when stage_progression_score ≥ 50 and probability ≥ 25; At Risk when stage_progression_score ≥ 30; otherwise Poor. - forecast_accuracy_days: for closed opportunities with close_date on or before today, return the non-negative number of days between close_date and today; otherwise NULL. - is_current_quarter_close: true when close_date falls in the same calendar quarter and year as today; false otherwise (including NULL close_date). - is_current_quarter_created: true when created_date falls in the same calendar quarter and year as today; false otherwise (including NULL created_date). - opportunity_status: Won if is_won; Lost if is_closed and not is_won; Open if not is_closed; otherwise Unknown. - deal_cycle_category: Quick (≤30 days), Standard (31–90 days), Extended (91–180 days), or Long Cycle (>180 days) based on opportunity_age_days; thresholds are inclusive. - Output all selected columns; fields from non-matching joins are NULL; no additional filtering or de-duplication.'
      columns:
      - name: opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce opportunity identifier; duplicates preserved and NULLs passed through
      - name: opportunity_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity name from source; casing and whitespace preserved; NULLs passed through
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account identifier from op used to join ae; NULLs passed through; unmatched ae fields remain NULL
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: account_size_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account size segment from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: is_high_value_account
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: From ae; NULL when no account_enhanced match
      - name: geographic_region
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From ae; NULL when no account_enhanced match
      - name: industry_normalized
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From ae; NULL when no account_enhanced match
      - name: stage_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Current opportunity stage from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: stage_progression_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Stage progression score from op; used in pipeline_health_score; NULLs passed through
      - name: amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Two-decimal opportunity amount from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: deal_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Deal size category from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: expected_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(15,2)
        description: Two-decimal expected revenue from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: probability
        data_type: DECIMAL(5,2)
        description: Win probability from op; used in pipeline_health_score; NULLs passed through
      - name: weighted_pipeline_value
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Weighted pipeline value from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: win_probability_confidence
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Confidence descriptor for probability from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: pipeline_health_score
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Derived status: Healthy, Moderate, At Risk, or Poor based on stage_progression_score, probability, and deal_velocity_risk; never NULL'
      - name: is_closed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Closed flag from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: is_won
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Won flag from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: opportunity_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Won if is_won; Lost if is_closed and not is_won; Open if not is_closed; Unknown otherwise
      - name: close_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Opportunity close date from op; used for date parts and flags; NULLs passed through
      - name: close_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar year of close_date; NULL when close_date is NULL
      - name: close_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter of close_date; NULL when close_date is NULL
      - name: close_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar month of close_date; NULL when close_date is NULL
      - name: fiscal_quarter
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal quarter from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: fiscal_year
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal year from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: opportunity_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Age in days from op; used for deal_cycle_category; NULLs passed through
      - name: deal_cycle_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Derived from opportunity_age_days: ≤30 Quick, 31–90 Standard, 91–180 Extended, >180 Long Cycle; thresholds inclusive'
      - name: deal_velocity_risk
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Velocity risk classification from op; used by pipeline_health_score; NULLs passed through
      - name: close_date_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Close date status from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: days_to_close
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days to close from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: forecast_accuracy_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: For closed deals with close_date on or before today, the non-negative day difference between close_date and today; else NULL
      - name: is_strategic_deal
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Strategic deal flag from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: forecast_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Forecast category from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead source from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Campaign identifier from op; NULLs passed through
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity owner identifier from op used to join active users; NULLs passed through
      - name: owner_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'From active users: concatenated and trimmed first and last name; NULL when no active user match; empty string when both names are missing'
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Opportunity creation timestamp from op; used for date parts and flags; NULLs passed through
      - name: created_year

        description: Calendar year of created_date; NULL when created_date is NULL

        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter of created_date; NULL when created_date is NULL
      - name: created_month
        data_type: BIGINT

      - name: is_current_quarter_close
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when close_date is in the current calendar quarter and year; false otherwise
      - name: is_current_quarter_created
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when created_date is in the current calendar quarter and year; false otherwise
      - name: billing_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
