## fct_salesforce__lead_performance

    - name: fct_salesforce__lead_performance
      description: Lead conversion performance per lead with derived owner name, conversion flags, and created_date-based time attributes.
      grain: One row per lead_id
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__lead_conversion_funnel
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      business_logic: '- Start from intermediate.int_salesforce__lead_conversion_funnel (lf); no filtering or aggregation; output grain is one row per lead_id. - Build an active user lookup from staging.stg_salesforce__user where is_active = true; compute owner_name by concatenating first_name and last_name with a single space, treating missing parts as empty, and trimming extra spaces. - Left join users to lf on lf.owner_id = user_id; if no active user matches, owner_name is NULL. - Derive conversion_rate as an integer flag: 1 when lf.is_converted is true, else 0. - Derive converted_to_opportunity as true when lf.converted_opportunity_id is present, else false. - Derive time attributes from lf.created_date: created_year (calendar year), created_quarter (1–4), created_month (1–12), created_week (ISO week 1–53). - Derive period flags relative to the current_date at runtime: - is_current_month is true when created_date falls in the current calendar month and year; otherwise false (also false if created_date is NULL). - is_current_quarter is true when created_date falls in the current calendar quarter and year; otherwise false (also false if created_date is NULL). - All other fields are passed through from lf unchanged.'
      columns:
      - name: lead_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique lead identifier; the result has one row per lead_id.
      - name: lead_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead display name from the funnel source.
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead first name from the funnel source.
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead last name from the funnel source.
      - name: company
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead company name from the funnel source.
      - name: email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead email address from the funnel source.
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Original lead source from the funnel source.
      - name: lead_source_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Categorized lead source from the funnel source.
      - name: lead_quality_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Numeric quality score from the funnel source.
      - name: lead_temperature
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead temperature label from the funnel source.
      - name: is_converted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when the lead is marked converted in the funnel source; else false.
      - name: conversion_rate
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Integer flag equal to 1 when is_converted is true, else 0; suitable for summing.
      - name: conversion_time_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Whole days between creation and conversion as provided by the funnel source; NULL if not applicable.
      - name: conversion_speed_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Bucketed conversion speed label from the funnel source.
      - name: has_complete_conversion
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when all funnel conversion steps are completed; else false.
      - name: converted_to_opportunity
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when converted_opportunity_id is present; else false.
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead owner user_id from the funnel source used to join to active users.
      - name: owner_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner full name from active users built as 'first_name last_name' with extra spaces trimmed; NULL if no active user match.
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Lead creation timestamp from the funnel source used for time derivations.
      - name: created_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar year derived from created_date.
      - name: created_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter (1–4) derived from created_date.
      - name: created_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar month (1–12) derived from created_date.
      - name: created_week
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: ISO calendar week number (1–53) derived from created_date.
      - name: status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead status from the funnel source.
      - name: industry
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead industry from the funnel source.
      - name: annual_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Annual revenue amount from the funnel source; precision 18, scale 2.
      - name: number_of_employees
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Employee count from the funnel source.
      - name: converted_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Timestamp when the lead was converted; may be NULL.
      - name: converted_account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account ID created by conversion, if any; NULL otherwise.
      - name: converted_contact_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Contact ID created by conversion, if any; NULL otherwise.
      - name: converted_opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity ID created by conversion, if any; NULL otherwise.
      - name: is_current_month
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when created_date falls in the current calendar month and year at runtime; else false or NULL treated as false.
      - name: is_current_quarter
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when created_date falls in the current calendar quarter and year at runtime; else false or NULL treated as false.


## fct_salesforce__account_engagement

    - name: fct_salesforce__account_engagement
      description: Account-level engagement, risk, lifecycle, and health metrics joined from account attributes, activity summaries, and active owner user info
      grain: One row per account_id from intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced (account_id, account_name, account_type, account_size_segment, geographic_region, industry_normalized, annual_revenue, account_age_days, account_maturity_stage, is_high_value_account, data_completeness_score, owner_id, billing_country, billing_state, billing_city, website)
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__activity_summary (account_id, total_activities_30d, total_activities_90d, engagement_temperature, activity_frequency_score, days_since_last_activity, unique_activity_owners, activity_trend_30d, is_declining_engagement)
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user (user_id, first_name, last_name, is_active)
      business_logic: '- Start from intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced (ae), one row per account_id. - Left join intermediate.int_salesforce__activity_summary (acs) on ae.account_id = acs.account_id. - Build owner_name from staging.stg_salesforce__user filtered to is_active=true by concatenating first and last name with a space and trimming; if no active user matches, owner_name is NULL; if both names are missing, owner_name is an empty string. - Compute engagement_health_score (0–100) by summing component points: - Engagement temperature: Hot=30, Warm=20, Cool=10, Cold=5, else 0. - Activity frequency: High=25, Medium=15, Low=8, else 3. - Unique activity owners: >=3 ->20; >=2 ->12; >=1 ->5; else 0. - 30d activity trend: Increasing=15, Stable=8, New=5, else 0. - Data completeness: >=80 ->10; >=60 ->6; >=40 ->3; else 0. - If a component value is NULL, it falls to the else bucket (activity frequency adds 3; others add 0). - Compute churn_risk_indicator: - High if temperature in (''Cold'',''Dormant'') AND trend=''Decreasing'' AND 90d activities < 5 - Else Medium if temperature=''Cool'' AND 90d activities < 10 - Else Low if temperature=''Warm'' AND frequency=''Low'' - Else Minimal - NULL inputs do not match any rule and return Minimal. - Compute customer_lifecycle_stage (first match wins): - Onboarding if maturity=''New'' AND 30d activities >= 5 - Active Growth if maturity in (''Growing'',''Established'') AND temperature in (''Hot'',''Warm'') - Stable Relationship if maturity=''Mature'' AND temperature in (''Hot'',''Warm'') - At Risk if temperature=''Cool'' - Dormant if temperature in (''Cold'',''Dormant'') - Else Unknown - Compute relationship_depth from acs.unique_activity_owners: - >=5: Deep; >=3: Moderate; >=2: Basic; >=1: Single Point; else: No Engagement; NULL behaves as else. - Compute has_upsell_opportunity (boolean): true if is_high_value_account=true AND temperature in (''Hot'',''Warm'') AND frequency in (''High'',''Medium''); else false. - Compute account_health_status by bucketing the same total used for engagement_health_score: - >=80: Excellent; >=60: Good; >=40: Fair; else: Poor. - Output columns: - Pass through ae attributes and owner_id; owner_name comes only from active users. - Apply defaults to activity fields in the final projection: - total_activities_30d, total_activities_90d, unique_activity_owners -> 0 - engagement_temperature -> ''Dormant'' - activity_frequency_score -> ''Minimal'' - activity_trend_30d -> ''New'' - is_declining_engagement -> false - days_since_last_activity is not defaulted and may be NULL.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Primary key of the account; exactly one output row per account_id
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name passed through from ae without transformation
      - name: account_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce account type passed through from ae
      - name: account_size_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Size segment classification from ae
      - name: geographic_region
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Geographic region designation from ae
      - name: industry_normalized
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Normalized industry label from ae
      - name: annual_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(15,2)
        description: Annual revenue from ae; two decimal places; passed through without additional rounding or defaults
      - name: account_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Age of the account in days from ae; may be NULL if not provided
      - name: account_maturity_stage
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account maturity stage from ae (e.g., New, Growing, Established, Mature)
      - name: is_high_value_account
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Flag from ae indicating high-value accounts; used in upsell logic
      - name: data_completeness_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 0–100 score from ae; if NULL it contributes 0 points in scoring
      - name: total_activities_30d
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of activities in last 30 days; defaulted to 0 when NULL
      - name: total_activities_90d
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of activities in last 90 days; defaulted to 0 when NULL
      - name: engagement_temperature
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Engagement temperature label; defaulted to 'Dormant' when NULL; referenced by scoring and risk/lifecycle rules
      - name: activity_frequency_score
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Frequency score label; defaulted to 'Minimal' when NULL; referenced by scoring and risk logic
      - name: days_since_last_activity
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days since most recent activity from acs; may be NULL if no activity exists
      - name: unique_activity_owners
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of activity owners; defaulted to 0 when NULL; scoring uses raw value where NULL contributes 0 points
      - name: activity_trend_30d
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 30-day activity trend label; defaulted to 'New' when NULL; used in scoring and risk logic
      - name: is_declining_engagement
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when engagement is declining per acs; defaulted to false when NULL
      - name: engagement_health_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 'Composite 0–100: temperature(30/20/10/5/0) + frequency(25/15/8/3) + owners(>=3:20,>=2:12,>=1:5,else 0) + trend(15/8/5/0) + data completeness(>=80:10,>=60:6,>=40:3,else 0); NULLs follow else (frequency adds 3; others add 0)'
      - name: churn_risk_indicator
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Risk bucket: High if cold/dormant AND decreasing AND 90d<5; else Medium if cool AND 90d<10; else Low if warm AND frequency=Low; else Minimal; NULLs fall through to Minimal'
      - name: customer_lifecycle_stage
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Lifecycle: Onboarding (New AND 30d>=5) > Active Growth (Growing/Established AND hot/warm) > Stable Relationship (Mature AND hot/warm) > At Risk (cool) > Dormant (cold/dormant) > Unknown; first matching rule applies'
      - name: relationship_depth
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Depth from unique_activity_owners: >=5 Deep, >=3 Moderate, >=2 Basic, >=1 Single Point, else No Engagement; NULL behaves as else'
      - name: has_upsell_opportunity
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if is_high_value_account=true AND temperature in ('Hot','Warm') AND frequency in ('High','Medium'); else false
      - name: account_health_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Bucketed health using the same score as engagement_health_score: >=80 Excellent, >=60 Good, >=40 Fair, else Poor (inclusive thresholds)'
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user ID from ae; may not have a matching active user
      - name: owner_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Active owner full name built by concatenating first and last name and trimming; NULL if no active match; empty string if both names are missing
      - name: billing_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing country from ae
      - name: billing_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing state from ae
      - name: billing_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing city from ae
      - name: website
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Website from ae; passed through without transformation


## dim_salesforce__user

    - name: dim_salesforce__user
      description: Salesforce user dimension with role, manager, tenure, and performance metrics
      grain: One row per user_id
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user_role
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance
      business_logic: 'Build a user dimension from Salesforce user, role, and user performance data: - Start with all users from staging.stg_salesforce__user (u). - Left join staging.stg_salesforce__user_role (ur) on user_role_id to enrich role attributes. - Left self-join staging.stg_salesforce__user (manager) on u.manager_id = manager.user_id to derive manager fields. - Left join intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance (up) on user_id to append performance metrics. - full_name: concatenate first_name and last_name with a single space, treating NULL as empty string, then trim whitespace from both ends; if both names are NULL, return an empty string. - user_type: case-insensitive classification based on user_role_name (first match wins): • contains "sales" or "account" → "Sales" • contains "marketing" → "Marketing" • contains "manager" or "director" or "vp" → "Management" • contains "support" or "service" → "Customer Success" • contains "admin" → "Administrator" • otherwise (including NULL role name) → "Other" - user_tenure_days: if last_login_date is present, compute the number of days between last_login_date (date only) and today; otherwise NULL. - user_tenure_category: if last_login_date is NULL → "Unknown"; else bucket by days since last_login_date: <90 → "Recent User"; <365 → "Active User"; otherwise → "Experienced User". - manager_id and manager_name: manager_id is manager.user_id from the self-join; manager_name is built like full_name from the manager’s names; if no manager match, manager_id is NULL and manager_name is an empty string. - productivity_level: map performance_tier to: • "High Performer" → "High Productivity" • "Good Performer" → "Good Productivity" • "Developing" → "Developing Productivity" • "New User" → "New User" • otherwise (including NULL) → "Unknown" - system_adoption_level: based on up.total_activities and up.total_opportunities: • at least 100 activities and at least 20 opportunities → "High Adoption" • at least 50 activities and at least 10 opportunities → "Medium Adoption" • at least 10 activities and at least 5 opportunities → "Basic Adoption" • more than 0 activities OR more than 0 opportunities → "Limited Adoption" • otherwise (including NULL metrics) → "No Activity" - activities_per_win_ratio: if total_activities > 0 and won_opportunities > 0, compute total_activities divided by won_opportunities and round to 2 decimal places; otherwise NULL. - All metrics from the performance model are passed through without transformation and remain NULL if no matching performance record exists.'
      columns:
      - name: user_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique Salesforce user identifier; one output row per user_id
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: username
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce username
      - name: email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: User email address
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: User first name
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: User last name
      - name: full_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Concatenation of first_name and last_name with a single space, trimming edges; NULL names treated as empty; both NULL → empty string
      - name: is_active
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: User active flag
      - name: user_role_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Foreign key to the user's Salesforce role
      - name: user_role_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: User role name from the role table; NULL if no matching role
      - name: role_developer_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Developer (API) name of the user role
      - name: parent_role_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Parent role id in the role hierarchy
      - name: rollup_description
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Role roll-up description from the hierarchy
      - name: profile_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce profile id assigned to the user
      - name: user_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Role-based category from substring rules on user_role_name (Sales, Marketing, Management, Customer Success, Administrator, Other)
      - name: user_tenure_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days from last_login_date to today; NULL if last_login_date is NULL
      - name: user_tenure_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Tenure bucket: Unknown (no last_login_date), Recent (<90 days), Active (<365 days), Experienced (≥365 days)'
      - name: manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager's user_id from the self-join; NULL if no matching manager
      - name: manager_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Concatenation of manager first_name and last_name, trimmed; empty string if no manager or both names are NULL
      - name: performance_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Performance tier from the performance model; may be NULL
      - name: productivity_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Derived from performance_tier: High/Good/Developing Productivity, New User, or Unknown'
      - name: system_adoption_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Derived from activity and opportunity thresholds in order: High, Medium, Basic, Limited, else No Activity'
      - name: total_opportunities
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total opportunities attributed to the user (pass-through)
      - name: won_opportunities
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total won opportunities attributed to the user (pass-through)
      - name: win_rate_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Opportunity win rate percentage (pass-through)
      - name: total_won_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of won opportunity amounts (pass-through)
      - name: total_pipeline_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Total pipeline amount (pass-through)
      - name: avg_won_deal_size
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average won deal size (pass-through)
      - name: total_leads
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total leads owned by the user (pass-through)
      - name: converted_leads
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total converted leads (pass-through)
      - name: lead_conversion_rate_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Lead conversion rate percentage (pass-through)
      - name: total_activities
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Total Salesforce activities completed or logged (pass-through)
      - name: activity_completion_rate_percentage
        data_type: DECIMAL(2,1)
        description: Activity completion rate percentage (pass-through)
      - name: activities_per_win_ratio
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: If total_activities > 0 and won_opportunities > 0, total_activities divided by won_opportunities rounded to 2 decimals; else NULL


## salesforce__account_daily_history

    - name: salesforce__account_daily_history
      description: Daily snapshots of Salesforce account history capturing, per account and day, the most recent non-deleted change.
      grain: One row per account_id per snapshot_date (latest non-deleted change on that day)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account_history
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_salesforce__account_history. - Keep only rows where is_deleted is FALSE; rows with is_deleted TRUE or NULL are excluded. - Derive snapshot_date by taking the calendar date from created_date (time removed). - For each (account_id, snapshot_date), sort records by created_date descending and select the first (latest) record for that day. - If multiple records share the exact same created_date timestamp within a day, no secondary tiebreaker is applied; any one of the tied rows may be chosen. - Output the listed fields directly from the chosen record; no transformations beyond deriving snapshot_date. - NULL values in selected fields are preserved; no standardization or unit changes. - Only days with at least one non-deleted record produce a row; no date backfilling. - Final output is ordered by account_id ascending, then snapshot_date ascending.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce account identifier for the snapshot.
      - name: snapshot_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date derived from created_date used to group daily changes.
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: account_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account source on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: industry
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Industry on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: annual_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Annual revenue on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: number_of_employees
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Employee count on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner ID on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account type on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: billing_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing city on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: billing_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing state on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: billing_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing country on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: website
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Website on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: rating
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Rating on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: ownership
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Ownership on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: parent_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Parent account ID on the selected latest change for that day.
      - name: last_activity_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Last activity date on the selected latest change for that day.

## salesforce__opportunity_daily_history

    - name: salesforce__opportunity_daily_history
      description: Daily latest-change snapshots of Salesforce opportunities derived from the history table (one row per opportunity per date)
      grain: One row per opportunity_id per snapshot_date (latest change on that date)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity_history
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity_history and exclude rows where is_deleted is true. - Derive snapshot_date by taking the date part of created_date (no timezone conversion). - Derive status in priority order: 1) Won when is_won is true. 2) Lost when is_won is false and is_closed is true. 3) Pipeline when is_closed is false and forecast_category equals any of ''pipeline'',''forecast'',''bestcase'' regardless of case. 4) Otherwise Other. - If is_won, is_closed, or forecast_category is NULL, none of the first three conditions apply and status is Other. - For each (opportunity_id, snapshot_date), rank rows by created_date descending and keep the row with rank = 1 (latest change that day). - If multiple rows share the same created_date within a day, one is chosen arbitrarily due to no secondary tie-breaker. - Output the selected row''s fields as listed; pass-through fields preserve source values (including NULLs, casing, and whitespace). - Order the final result by opportunity_id, then snapshot_date.'
      columns:
      - name: opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce Opportunity ID; unique with snapshot_date after daily dedup
      - name: snapshot_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date derived from created_date of the history row; one row per opportunity per day
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account ID from the opportunity history
      - name: opportunity_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity name from the opportunity history
      - name: amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Opportunity amount from the opportunity history
      - name: stage_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Stage name from the opportunity history
      - name: probability
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Probability from the opportunity history
      - name: close_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Close date from the opportunity history
      - name: is_closed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Closed flag from the opportunity history; used in status derivation
      - name: is_won
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Won flag from the opportunity history; used in status derivation
      - name: status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Categorical status derived from is_won, is_closed, and forecast_category: Won, Lost, Pipeline, or Other; NULL inputs result in Other; comparison to forecast_category is case-insensitive'
      - name: forecast_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Forecast category from the opportunity history; used in status derivation
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead source from the opportunity history
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity owner ID from the opportunity history

## salesforce__opportunity_enhanced

    - name: salesforce__opportunity_enhanced
      description: Enhanced Salesforce opportunity records joined to account, owner, manager, and role details with derived status and period metrics
      grain: One row per opportunity_id
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user_role
      business_logic: '- Base: start from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity; no filtering; emit all source columns as-is. - Enrichment: LEFT JOIN staging.stg_salesforce__account on opportunity.account_id = account.account_id to add account_number, account_source, industry, account_name, number_of_employees, and account_type (from account.type). - Enrichment: LEFT JOIN staging.stg_salesforce__user as opportunity_owner on opportunity.owner_id = opportunity_owner.user_id to add owner id, name, role_id, city, and state. - Enrichment: LEFT JOIN staging.stg_salesforce__user as opportunity_manager on opportunity_owner.manager_id = opportunity_manager.user_id to add manager id, name, city, and state. - Enrichment: LEFT JOIN staging.stg_salesforce__user_role on opportunity_owner.user_role_id = user_role.user_role_id to add opportunity_owner_position (role name), opportunity_owner_developer_name, opportunity_owner_parent_role_id, and opportunity_owner_rollup_description. - Derived status (evaluated in order): 1) if is_won is true → "Won" 2) else if is_closed is true and is_won is not true → "Lost" 3) else if is_closed is not true and forecast_category equals Pipeline, Forecast, or BestCase (case-insensitive) → "Pipeline" 4) else → "Other" Null booleans or NULL forecast_category do not meet conditions and result in "Other". - Derived period metrics: - created_amount_this_month / created_amount_this_quarter: return amount when the corresponding created flag is true (amount may be NULL), otherwise 0; NULL flags are treated as not true. - created_count_this_month / created_count_this_quarter: return 1 when the corresponding created flag is true, otherwise 0; NULL flags are treated as not true. - closed_amount_this_month / closed_amount_this_quarter: return amount when the corresponding closed flag is true (amount may be NULL), otherwise 0; NULL flags are treated as not true. - closed_count_this_month / closed_count_this_quarter: return 1 when the corresponding closed flag is true, otherwise 0; NULL flags are treated as not true. - Determinism: all joins are LEFT JOINs; unmatched enrichments yield NULLs; no aggregation, sorting, or de-duplication; pass-through values remain unchanged.'

## salesforce__owner_performance

    - name: salesforce__owner_performance
      description: Owner-level performance metrics projected 1:1 from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner
      grain: One row per owner_id
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner
      business_logic: '- Project the listed columns directly from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner with no filters, joins, recalculations, or re-ordering. - Grain: one row per owner_id as produced by the source model. - Numeric semantics: - DECIMAL(38,0): currency amounts in whole units, passed through without rounding or transformation. - DECIMAL(18,2): currency amounts with two decimal places, passed through. - DOUBLE: averages/durations as computed upstream; no additional rounding or casting. - BIGINT/HUGEINT: pre-aggregated counts; pass-through. - Time-sliced fields with suffixes "...this_month" and "...this_quarter" reflect the source period definitions; this model does not alter period cutoffs. - NULL policy: all columns are passed through exactly as in the source; NULLs are neither filled nor modified. - Ordering: no ORDER BY is applied; output row order is not guaranteed.'

## salesforce__daily_activity

    - name: salesforce__daily_activity
      description: Daily summary by date_day of leads created/converted, tasks/events counts, and opportunity creation/closure metrics with amounts rounded to 0 decimals.
      grain: One row per date_day
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__lead
      - staging.stg_salesforce__task
      - staging.stg_salesforce__event
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__date_spine
      business_logic: '- Start from the date spine (one row per date_day); this drives the output. - Tasks: group tasks by the calendar day of activity_date; tasks_completed is the number of non-null task records on that day; tasks with a null activity_date do not contribute and will not join; no additional filters. - Events: define each event’s activity day as the calendar day of activity_date, otherwise the calendar day of activity_date_time; if both are null the event is excluded; events_completed is the number of non-null event records on that day. - Leads created: group leads by the calendar day of created_date; leads_created is the number of non-null lead records on that day; rows with a null created_date are excluded. - Leads converted: restrict to leads where is_converted is true; group by the calendar day of converted_date; leads_converted is the number of non-null lead records on that day; rows with a null converted_date are excluded. - Opportunities base: for each opportunity derive created_day from created_date and close_day from close_date; set status as: • Won when is_won is true • Lost when is_won is false and is_closed is true • Pipeline when not closed and forecast_category (case-insensitive) is one of pipeline, forecast, bestcase • Other otherwise Amount may be null and is used in sums. - Opportunities created (by created_day): • opportunities_created: number of non-null opportunity records • opportunities_created_amount: sum of amount rounded to 0 decimals; sums ignore null amounts and are null when all amounts are null - Opportunities closed (by close_day): • opportunities_won: number of opportunities with status Won; zero when there are close_day records but none Won; null when no close_day records • opportunities_won_amount: sum of amounts for status Won rounded to 0 decimals; zero when there are close_day records but none Won; null when no close_day records • opportunities_lost: number of opportunities with status Lost; zero when there are close_day records but none Lost; null when no close_day records • opportunities_lost_amount: sum of amounts for status Lost rounded to 0 decimals; null when there are close_day records but none Lost; null when no close_day records • pipeline_amount: sum of amounts for status Pipeline rounded to 0 decimals; null when there are close_day records but none Pipeline; null when no close_day records - Output: left join each aggregate to the date spine on date_day; if a date has no matching aggregate row, the corresponding fields remain null; no defaulting to zero beyond the rules above.'
      columns:
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar date from the date spine and join key; exactly one row per date_day.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: leads_created
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of leads created on date_day based on created_date; only non-null lead records counted; null when no leads match that day.
      - name: leads_converted
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of leads with is_converted=true whose converted_date falls on date_day; only non-null lead records counted; null when none match that day.
      - name: tasks_completed
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of tasks whose activity_date falls on date_day; tasks with null activity_date are excluded; null when none match that day.
      - name: events_completed
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of events by day using activity_date when present else activity_date_time; events with both timestamps null are excluded; null when none match that day.
      - name: opportunities_created
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of opportunities created on date_day (by created_date); only non-null records counted; null when none match that day.
      - name: opportunities_created_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities created on date_day, rounded to 0 decimals; null when all amounts are null or no records match that day.
      - name: opportunities_won
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of opportunities with close_date on date_day and status Won; zero when close_date records exist but none Won; null when no close_date records.
      - name: opportunities_won_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities with close_date on date_day and status Won, rounded to 0 decimals; zero when close_date records exist but none Won; null when no close_date records.
      - name: opportunities_lost
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of opportunities with close_date on date_day and status Lost; zero when close_date records exist but none Lost; null when no close_date records.
      - name: opportunities_lost_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities with close_date on date_day and status Lost, rounded to 0 decimals; null when close_date records exist but none Lost or when no close_date records.
      - name: pipeline_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of amounts for opportunities with close_date on date_day and status Pipeline, rounded to 0 decimals; null when close_date records exist but none Pipeline or when no close_date records.


## salesforce__opportunity_line_item_enhanced

    - name: salesforce__opportunity_line_item_enhanced
      description: Opportunity line items enriched with related opportunity, product, account, and owner details, plus a derived status.
      grain: One row per opportunity_line_item_id
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity_line_item
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - staging.stg_salesforce__product_2
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      business_logic: '- Start with all columns from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity_line_item (aliased oli) as the base rows. - Left join staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity (opp) on oli.opportunity_id = opp.opportunity_id; select opportunity_name, account_id, owner_id renamed to opportunity_owner_id, stage_name, close_date, is_won, is_closed, forecast_category. - Left join staging.stg_salesforce__product_2 (product) on oli.product_2_id = product.product_2_id; select product_name, product_code, family renamed to product_family. - Left join staging.stg_salesforce__account (account) on opp.account_id = account.account_id; select account_name and industry renamed to account_industry. - Left join staging.stg_salesforce__user (usr) on opp.owner_id = usr.user_id; select user_name renamed to opportunity_owner_name and email renamed to opportunity_owner_email. - Derive opportunity_status in this priority: 1) if is_won is true → "Won"; 2) else if is_won is false and is_closed is true → "Lost"; 3) else if is_closed is false and forecast_category equals any of "pipeline", "forecast", or "bestcase" when compared case-insensitively → "Pipeline"; 4) else → "Other". Any NULL in is_won, is_closed, or forecast_category prevents a match and defaults to "Other". - No filters or aggregations; each opportunity line item produces one output row. Unmatched joins leave the joined columns as NULL.'
      columns:
      - name: opportunity_line_item_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Primary key of the opportunity line item; exactly one output row per value; NULLs not allowed.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Passthrough from oli.opportunity_id; join key to opportunity; NULLs preserved.
      - name: product_2_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Passthrough from oli.product_2_id; join key to product; NULLs preserved.
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Passthrough from oli.created_date; timestamp as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: opportunity_line_item_description
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Passthrough from oli.opportunity_line_item_description; text as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: has_quantity_schedule
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Passthrough from oli.has_quantity_schedule; boolean as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: has_revenue_schedule
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Passthrough from oli.has_revenue_schedule; boolean as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: is_deleted
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Passthrough from oli.is_deleted; soft-delete flag; NULLs preserved.
      - name: last_modified_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Passthrough from oli.last_modified_date; timestamp as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: list_price
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Passthrough from oli.list_price; decimal scale 3 as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: pricebook_entry_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Passthrough from oli.pricebook_entry_id; text as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: quantity
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Passthrough from oli.quantity; decimal scale 3 as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: service_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Passthrough from oli.service_date; date as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: sort_order
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Passthrough from oli.sort_order; integer as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: total_price
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Passthrough from oli.total_price; decimal scale 3 as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: unit_price
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,3)
        description: Passthrough from oli.unit_price; decimal scale 3 as stored; NULLs preserved.
      - name: opportunity_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From opp.opportunity_name via left join on opportunity_id; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From opp.account_id via left join; join key to account; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: opportunity_owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From opp.owner_id via left join; join key to user; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: stage_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From opp.stage_name via left join; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: close_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: From opp.close_date via left join; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: is_won
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: From opp.is_won via left join; drives opportunity_status; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: is_closed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: From opp.is_closed via left join; drives opportunity_status; NULL if no matching opportunity.
      - name: forecast_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From opp.forecast_category via left join; compared case-insensitively when deriving opportunity_status; NULL if no match.
      - name: product_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From product.product_name via left join on product_2_id; NULL if no matching product.
      - name: product_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From product.product_code via left join on product_2_id; NULL if no matching product.
      - name: product_family
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From product.family via left join on product_2_id; NULL if no matching product.
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From account.account_name via left join on opp.account_id; NULL if no matching account.
      - name: account_industry
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From account.industry via left join on opp.account_id; NULL if no matching account.
      - name: opportunity_owner_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From usr.user_name via left join on opp.owner_id; NULL if no matching user.
      - name: opportunity_owner_email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: From usr.email via left join on opp.owner_id; NULL if no matching user.
      - name: opportunity_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Derived status: Won if is_won is true; Lost if is_won is false and is_closed is true; Pipeline if is_closed is false and forecast_category equals Pipeline, Forecast, or BestCase case-insensitively; otherwise Other; NULLs default to Other.'


## salesforce__sales_snapshot

    - name: salesforce__sales_snapshot
      description: Daily sales snapshot computed at run time with aggregated opportunity, lead, account, and user KPIs
      grain: One row per snapshot_date (current_date at execution)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
      - staging.stg_salesforce__lead
      - staging.stg_salesforce__account
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      business_logic: 'Snapshot context: - Set snapshot_date to the execution date. - Set current_month_start to the first calendar day of the current month. - Set current_quarter_start to the first calendar day of the current quarter. - Set current_year_start to the first calendar day of the current year. Opportunity metrics (from opportunities excluding deleted records): - total_opportunities: count of all rows. - won_opportunities: count of rows marked won. - lost_opportunities: count of rows closed and not won. - open_opportunities: count of rows not closed. - total_opportunity_value: sum of amount across all rows. - won_amount: sum of amount for won rows; non-matching rows contribute 0. - lost_amount: sum of amount for closed-not-won rows; non-matching rows contribute 0. - pipeline_amount: sum of amount for open (not closed) rows; non-matching rows contribute 0. - avg_days_to_close: average days_to_close over closed rows; ignores missing values. - avg_won_deal_size: average amount over won rows; ignores missing values. - opportunities_created_this_month: count of rows created this month. - opportunities_created_this_quarter: count of rows created this quarter. - opportunities_won_this_month: count of rows both won and closed this month. - opportunities_won_this_quarter: count of rows both won and closed this quarter. - pipeline_created_this_month: sum of amount for rows created this month; non-matching rows contribute 0. - pipeline_created_this_quarter: sum of amount for rows created this quarter; non-matching rows contribute 0. - revenue_this_month: sum of amount for rows both won and closed this month; non-matching rows contribute 0. - revenue_this_quarter: sum of amount for rows both won and closed this quarter; non-matching rows contribute 0. Lead metrics (from leads excluding deleted records): - total_leads: count of all rows. - converted_leads: count of rows marked converted. - open_leads: count of rows not converted. - conversion_rate_pct: converted_leads divided by total_leads times 100, rounded to two decimals; when there are zero leads, the result is NULL. Account metrics (from accounts excluding deleted records): - total_accounts: count of all rows. - unique_account_owners: distinct count of owner_id. - unique_industries: distinct count of industry. - avg_company_size: average number_of_employees; ignores missing values. User metrics (from users, no deletion filter): - total_users: count of all rows. - active_users: count of rows marked active. Final assembly and calculated rates: - Cross join the single-row metrics above with the snapshot context to return exactly one row. - win_rate_pct: if total_opportunities > 0, won_opportunities divided by total_opportunities times 100, rounded to two decimals; otherwise 0. - close_rate_pct: if total_opportunities > 0, (won_opportunities + lost_opportunities) divided by total_opportunities times 100, rounded to two decimals; otherwise 0. Null and empty-set behavior: - Counts return 0 when there are no rows; conditional counts include only rows where the condition is true. - Conditional sums treat non-matching rows as zero; if the table has no rows, these sums are NULL. - Plain sums return NULL when there are no rows or all amounts are missing; averages ignore missing values and return NULL when no qualifying rows exist. - Percentage metrics are rounded to two decimals; currency amounts retain two-decimal scale.'
      columns:
      - name: snapshot_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Execution date of the snapshot
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: current_month_start
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the month containing snapshot_date
      - name: current_quarter_start
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the quarter containing snapshot_date
      - name: current_year_start
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the year containing snapshot_date
      - name: total_opportunities
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Row count of opportunities excluding deleted
      - name: won_opportunities
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities marked won
      - name: lost_opportunities
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities closed and not won
      - name: open_opportunities
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities not closed
      - name: total_opportunity_value
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount across opportunities excluding deleted; NULL if no rows or all amounts are NULL
      - name: won_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for won opportunities; 0 when rows exist but none are won; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: lost_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for closed-not-won opportunities; 0 when rows exist but none match; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: pipeline_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for open opportunities; 0 when rows exist but none are open; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: avg_days_to_close
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days_to_close over closed opportunities; ignores NULL; NULL if none
      - name: avg_won_deal_size
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average amount over won opportunities; ignores NULL; NULL if none
      - name: opportunities_created_this_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities created this month
      - name: opportunities_created_this_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities created this quarter
      - name: opportunities_won_this_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities won and closed this month
      - name: opportunities_won_this_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of opportunities won and closed this quarter
      - name: pipeline_created_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for opportunities created this month; 0 when rows exist but none match; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: pipeline_created_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for opportunities created this quarter; 0 when rows exist but none match; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: revenue_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for opportunities won and closed this month; 0 when rows exist but none match; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: revenue_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Sum of amount for opportunities won and closed this quarter; 0 when rows exist but none match; NULL if no opportunity rows
      - name: total_leads
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Row count of leads excluding deleted
      - name: converted_leads
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of leads marked converted
      - name: open_leads
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of leads not converted
      - name: conversion_rate_pct
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Converted leads as a percentage of total leads, rounded to two decimals; NULL when there are zero leads
      - name: total_accounts
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Row count of accounts excluding deleted
      - name: unique_account_owners
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of account owner_id among accounts excluding deleted
      - name: unique_industries
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of industry among accounts excluding deleted
      - name: avg_company_size
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average employees per account excluding deleted; ignores NULL; NULL if none
      - name: total_users
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Row count of users
      - name: active_users
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of users marked active
      - name: win_rate_pct
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Won opportunities as a percentage of total opportunities, rounded to two decimals; 0 when there are zero opportunities
      - name: close_rate_pct
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Closed (won or lost) opportunities as a percentage of total opportunities, rounded to two decimals; 0 when there are zero opportunities


## salesforce__manager_performance

    - name: salesforce__manager_performance
      description: Manager-level aggregation of team performance metrics joined with manager profile and derived ratios
      grain: One row per manager_id
      source_models:
      - marts.salesforce__owner_performance
      - staging.stg_salesforce__user
      business_logic: '- Start from marts.salesforce__owner_performance and keep rows with a non-null manager_id. - Aggregate by manager_id: - total_team_members is the count of distinct owner_id values (nulls excluded). - For all team amounts and counts listed in the columns (excluding averages and maxima), sum the corresponding owner-level values after replacing nulls with 0; if all inputs are null, the result is 0. - team_avg_days_to_close is the simple average of owner-level avg_days_to_close after replacing nulls with 0; unweighted by deal counts; no rounding applied. - team_largest_booking and team_largest_pipeline_deal are the maximum owner-level values after treating nulls as 0; if all inputs are null, the result is 0. - Enrich with manager profile by left joining staging.stg_salesforce__user on user_id = manager_id; unmatched profile fields remain null. - Derive ratios: - win_loss_ratio is team_total_bookings_amount divided by team_total_lost_amount, rounded to 2 decimals, only when both amounts are greater than 0; otherwise null. - avg_bookings_per_rep is team_total_bookings_amount divided by total_team_members, rounded to 2 decimals, only when total_team_members is greater than 0; otherwise 0. - Output one row per manager_id; the final result is ordered by team_total_bookings_amount descending (ordering does not change values).'
      columns:
      - name: manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager identifier; grouping key for all metrics
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: total_team_members
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of owner_id under the manager; null owner_id excluded; duplicates collapsed
      - name: team_bookings_amount_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of bookings_amount_closed_this_month across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_bookings_amount_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of bookings_amount_closed_this_quarter across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_bookings
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of total_number_bookings across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_bookings_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of total_bookings_amount across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_lost_amount_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of lost_amount_this_month across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_lost_amount_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of lost_amount_this_quarter across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_lost
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of total_number_lost across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_lost_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of total_lost_amount across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_pipeline_created_this_month
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of pipeline_created_amount_this_month across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_pipeline_created_this_quarter
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of pipeline_created_amount_this_quarter across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_pipeline
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of total_number_pipeline across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_total_pipeline_amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,0)
        description: Sum of total_pipeline_amount across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0
      - name: team_avg_days_to_close
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Simple average of avg_days_to_close across owners with nulls treated as 0; unweighted; no rounding
      - name: team_largest_booking
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Maximum largest_booking across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0; if all nulls then 0.00
      - name: team_largest_pipeline_deal
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Maximum largest_deal_in_pipeline across the manager’s owners with nulls treated as 0; if all nulls then 0.00
      - name: manager_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager full name from stg_salesforce__user.user_name; null if no user match
      - name: manager_email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager email from stg_salesforce__user.email; null if no user match
      - name: manager_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager title from stg_salesforce__user.title; null if no user match
      - name: manager_department
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager department from stg_salesforce__user.department; null if no user match
      - name: manager_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager city from stg_salesforce__user.city; null if no user match
      - name: manager_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager state from stg_salesforce__user.state; null if no user match
      - name: win_loss_ratio
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: team_total_bookings_amount ÷ team_total_lost_amount, rounded to 2 decimals, only when both > 0; else null
      - name: avg_bookings_per_rep
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: team_total_bookings_amount ÷ total_team_members, rounded to 2 decimals, only when total_team_members > 0; else 0


## salesforce__revenue_analytics

    - name: salesforce__revenue_analytics
      description: Closed-opportunity revenue analytics with temporal, velocity, seasonal, and account-value metrics (one row per closed opportunity)
      grain: One row per opportunity_id (closed opportunities only; no de-duplication)
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced
      - marts.dim_salesforce__user
      business_logic: '- Start from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline and keep only rows where is_closed = true. - Left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced on account_id to add account_size_segment, geographic_region, industry_normalized, and is_high_value_account. - Left join marts.dim_salesforce__user on owner_id = user_id to add owner_name, owner_type, owner_performance_tier, and owner_manager. - Select baseline fields from the opportunity (ids, names, stage, amount, deal_size_category, is_won, close_date, fiscal_quarter, fiscal_year, opportunity_age_days as sales_cycle_days, lead_source, owner_id, created_date) plus account/user enrichments. - Revenue fields: - won_revenue equals amount when is_won = true, else 0; if amount is NULL in the true branch, won_revenue is NULL. - lost_revenue equals amount when is_won = false, else 0; if amount is NULL in the true branch, lost_revenue is NULL. - Calendar parts: derive close_year, close_quarter (1–4), and close_month (1–12) from close_date; these are NULL when close_date is NULL. - revenue_per_day: if is_won = true and sales_cycle_days > 0, compute amount divided by sales_cycle_days, rounded to 2 decimals; otherwise 0. If amount is NULL in the true branch, the result is NULL. - revenue_velocity_category: - Wins: ≤30 days → "Fast Win (≤30d)"; 31–90 → "Standard Win (31-90d)"; 91–180 → "Extended Win (91-180d)"; >180 → "Long Cycle Win (>180d)". - Losses: ≤30 days → "Fast Loss (≤30d)"; 31–90 → "Standard Loss (31-90d)"; 91–180 → "Extended Loss (91-180d)"; >180 → "Long Cycle Loss (>180d)". - Quarter flags relative to current_date at run time: - is_current_quarter is true when close_date falls in the same calendar year and quarter as current_date; false otherwise. - is_previous_quarter is true for the immediately prior calendar quarter (same year with current_quarter-1, or when current quarter is Q1 then prior year Q4); false otherwise. - Seasonal fields: seasonal_quarter equals the calendar quarter of close_date; seasonal_period labels quarters as "Q1 - New Year Start", "Q2 - Mid Year Push", "Q3 - Summer Deals", "Q4 - Year End Close"; NULL when close_date is NULL. - Account value outcome precedence: if is_high_value_account = true and is_won then "High Value Account Win"; if is_high_value_account = true and not is_won then "High Value Account Loss"; else if account_size_segment = "Enterprise" and is_won then "Enterprise Win"; else if account_size_segment = "Enterprise" and not is_won then "Enterprise Loss"; else "Standard Deal". - Grain: relies on unique opportunity_id in the pipeline and one-to-one joins on account_id and owner_id; no aggregation or duplicate removal is applied.'
      columns:
      - name: opportunity_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce opportunity unique identifier; exactly one output row per id (closed opportunities only)
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: opportunity_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity name from pipeline; passed through as-is; may be NULL
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Salesforce account identifier from pipeline; used to join account_enhanced; may be NULL
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from pipeline; passed through as-is; may be NULL
      - name: account_size_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account size segment from account_enhanced via left join; NULL when no account match
      - name: geographic_region
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Geographic region from account_enhanced via left join; NULL when no account match
      - name: industry_normalized
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Normalized industry from account_enhanced via left join; NULL when no account match
      - name: stage_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Final stage name at close from pipeline; passed through as-is
      - name: amount
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Opportunity amount from pipeline; no rounding applied here (precision 18,2); may be NULL
      - name: deal_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Upstream deal size bucket from pipeline; passed through as-is
      - name: is_won
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if the closed opportunity is a win; false for a loss; sourced from pipeline
      - name: won_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Amount when is_won = true else 0; NULL when amount is NULL in the win case
      - name: lost_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Amount when is_won = false else 0; NULL when amount is NULL in the loss case
      - name: close_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Opportunity close date from pipeline; used for all date-derived fields; may be NULL
      - name: close_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar year derived from close_date; NULL if close_date is NULL
      - name: close_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter (1–4) derived from close_date; NULL if close_date is NULL
      - name: close_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar month (1–12) derived from close_date; NULL if close_date is NULL
      - name: fiscal_quarter
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal quarter from pipeline; passed through as-is; may differ from calendar quarter
      - name: fiscal_year
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Fiscal year from pipeline; passed through as-is
      - name: sales_cycle_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: opportunity_age_days from pipeline; number of days from creation to close; may be NULL
      - name: revenue_per_day
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: If is_won = true and sales_cycle_days > 0 then amount divided by sales_cycle_days rounded to 2 decimals; NULL if amount is NULL in the true branch; otherwise 0
      - name: revenue_velocity_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Categorical label by is_won and sales_cycle_days: Fast/Standard/Extended/Long Cycle Win or Loss using thresholds ≤30, ≤90, ≤180, >180'
      - name: seasonal_quarter
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Calendar quarter (1–4) from close_date (duplicate of close_quarter); NULL if close_date is NULL
      - name: seasonal_period
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Quarter label from close_date quarter: Q1 - New Year Start, Q2 - Mid Year Push, Q3 - Summer Deals, Q4 - Year End Close; NULL if close_date is NULL'
      - name: is_current_quarter
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when close_date is in the current calendar quarter of current_date; false otherwise or if close_date is NULL
      - name: is_previous_quarter
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True when close_date is in the previous calendar quarter relative to current_date, with Q1 rolling back to prior year Q4; false otherwise or if close_date is NULL
      - name: account_value_outcome
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Outcome priority: High Value Account Win/Loss if is_high_value_account=true; else Enterprise Win/Loss if account_size_segment=''Enterprise''; else Standard Deal'
      - name: lead_source
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lead source from pipeline; passed through as-is
      - name: owner_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Opportunity owner user_id from pipeline; join key to dim_salesforce__user
      - name: owner_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner full name from dim_salesforce__user via left join; NULL if no user match
      - name: owner_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner user_type from dim_salesforce__user via left join; NULL if no user match
      - name: owner_performance_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner performance_tier from dim_salesforce__user via left join; NULL if no user match
      - name: owner_manager
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Owner manager_name from dim_salesforce__user via left join; NULL if no user match
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Opportunity created timestamp from pipeline; passed through as-is


## salesforce__team_performance

    - name: salesforce__team_performance
      description: Aggregated Salesforce team metrics by manager and team type for active users, including tiers, health score, and recent performance
      grain: One row per team_manager_id + team_manager_name + team_type
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance
      - marts.dim_salesforce__user
      - marts.salesforce__revenue_analytics
      business_logic: '- Create user_performance from intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance and user_dimension from marts.dim_salesforce__user. - From marts.salesforce__revenue_analytics, aggregate per owner_id: total revenue won (sum of won_revenue), deals won (count of rows where won), average sales cycle (average days on won deals), current-quarter won revenue (sum where in current quarter), previous-quarter won revenue (sum where in previous quarter), and current-quarter deals won (count of won deals in current quarter). - Join user_dimension to user_performance on user_id (left join), and to revenue_data on user_id = owner_id (left join). Keep only rows where the user is active, then group by: • team_manager_id = manager_id with nulls replaced by "No Manager" • team_manager_name = manager_name with nulls replaced by "No Manager" • team_type = user_type (can be null) - For each group, compute: • team_size = distinct count of users • active_team_members = distinct count of users where active (equals team_size under the active-user filter) • high_performers, good_performers, developing_performers, new_users = distinct user counts by performance_tier exact match • team_total_opportunities and team_won_opportunities = sums across users • team_total_revenue and team_total_pipeline = sums of revenue and pipeline amounts • team_win_rate_percentage = 100 × (total won opportunities ÷ total opportunities), rounded to two decimals; return 0 if total opportunities is zero or null • avg_opportunities_per_member and avg_wins_per_member = per-user averages, rounded to one decimal; nulls excluded • avg_revenue_per_member = per-user average revenue won, rounded to the nearest whole unit; nulls excluded • avg_win_rate_per_member = per-user average win rate percentage, rounded to two decimals; nulls excluded • team_total_leads and team_converted_leads = sums across users • team_lead_conversion_rate = 100 × (converted leads ÷ total leads), rounded to two decimals; return 0 if total leads is zero or null • team_total_activities = sum across users; avg_activity_completion_rate = per-user average completion rate percentage, rounded to two decimals; nulls excluded • team_revenue_current_quarter and team_deals_current_quarter = sums of current-quarter revenue and deals from revenue_data • revenue_growth_qoq_percentage = 100 × (current-quarter revenue minus previous-quarter revenue) ÷ previous-quarter revenue, rounded to two decimals; null if previous-quarter revenue is zero or null • team_activities_per_win_ratio = total activities ÷ total won opportunities, rounded to two decimals; null unless both totals are greater than zero • top_revenue_performer = the full_name of the member with the highest total won amount; members without a value are ranked last; ties are arbitrary • top_win_rate_performer = the full_name of the member with the highest win rate percentage; members without a value are ranked last; ties are arbitrary • avg_team_tenure_days = average user tenure in days, rounded to the nearest whole day; nulls excluded • new_team_members and veteran_team_members = distinct user counts where user_tenure_category is "New User" or "Veteran" - Enhance with: • team_performance_tier: - High Performing Team: win rate ≥ 30, average revenue per member ≥ 100,000, and at least 2 high performers - Good Performing Team: win rate ≥ 20, average revenue per member ≥ 50,000, and high + good performers cover at least half of the team - Developing Team: win rate ≥ 10 and average revenue per member ≥ 25,000 - Otherwise: Needs Improvement • team_health_score = sum of five components, clipped to the 0–100 range: - Win rate contribution: the win rate capped at 25 - High-performer share: 25 if high performers ÷ team_size ≥ 0.3, else 15 if ≥ 0.1, else 5 (when team_size is 0, this defaults to 5) - Activity completion: 20 if ≥ 80%, else 15 if ≥ 60%, else 10 - Revenue growth: 20 if > 10%, else 15 if > 0%, else 10 if ≥ -10%, else 5 - Lead conversion: 10 if ≥ 20%, else 7 if ≥ 10%, else 3 • team_diversity_level: - High Diversity: team_size ≥ 5 and at least one high, one good, and one developing performer - Medium Diversity: team_size ≥ 3 and at least one high or good performer and at least one developing performer - Low Diversity: team_size ≥ 2 - Otherwise: Single Member - Output all fields from the enhanced dataset; final ordering is by team_health_score descending, then team_total_revenue descending.'
      columns:
      - name: team_manager_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager identifier for the team with nulls replaced by 'No Manager'.
      - name: team_manager_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Manager full name with nulls replaced by 'No Manager'.
      - name: team_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Team type from user_type; can be null and is part of the grouping.
      - name: team_size
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active users in the team after filtering to active users.
      - name: active_team_members
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active users; equals team_size under the active-user filter.
      - name: high_performers
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct user count with performance_tier equal to 'High Performer'.
      - name: good_performers
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct user count with performance_tier equal to 'Good Performer'.
      - name: developing_performers
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct user count with performance_tier equal to 'Developing'.
      - name: new_users
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct user count with performance_tier equal to 'New User'.
      - name: team_performance_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Team tier based on win rate, average revenue per member, and performer mix thresholds.
      - name: team_health_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Composite score from five components capped between 0 and 100.
      - name: team_diversity_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Diversity category based on team size and presence across performance tiers.
      - name: team_total_opportunities
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total opportunities summed across team; nulls ignored and result is null if all inputs are null.
      - name: team_won_opportunities
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total won opportunities summed across team; nulls ignored and result is null if all inputs are null.
      - name: team_win_rate_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Win rate as 100 × won opportunities ÷ total opportunities, rounded to two decimals; returns 0 if total opportunities is zero or null.
      - name: team_total_revenue
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Total revenue won summed across team; currency formatted to two decimals; nulls ignored and result is null if all inputs are null.
      - name: team_total_pipeline
        data_type: DECIMAL(38,2)
        description: Total pipeline amount summed across team; currency formatted to two decimals; nulls ignored and result is null if all inputs are null.
      - name: avg_opportunities_per_member
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average opportunities per user rounded to one decimal; excludes nulls and is null if no values.
      - name: avg_wins_per_member
