    - salesforce__opportunity_daily_history#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os
import yaml
import duckdb
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
import logging


logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SalesforceDataPipeline:
    """Salesforce Data Pipeline Orchestrator - Supports a layered Schema architecture"""
    
    def __init__(self, 
                 source_db_path: str = "salesforce_start.duckdb", 
                 target_db_path: str = "salesforce.duckdb",
                 config_path: str = "config/layer_dependencies.yaml"):
        self.source_db_path = source_db_path
        self.target_db_path = target_db_path
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.config = self._load_config()
        self.sql_dir = Path("sql")
        
                
        self.schema_config = {
            'raw': 'raw',                       
            'staging': 'staging',               
            'intermediate': 'intermediate',             
            'marts': 'marts'                     
        }
        
                
        self.conn = None
        
    def _load_config(self) -> dict:
        """Loads the configuration file."""
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return yaml.safe_load(f)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            logger.error(f"Configuration file {self.config_path} not found")
            raise
        except yaml.YAMLError as e:
            logger.error(f"Error parsing configuration file: {e}")
            raise
    
    def _initialize_database(self):
        """Initializes the working database, creates the layered schema architecture, and imports raw data."""
        logger.info(f"📥 Creating new layered database: {self.target_db_path}")
        
                
        if not os.path.exists(self.source_db_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source database file not found: {self.source_db_path}")
        
        self.conn = duckdb.connect(self.target_db_path)
        
                
        self._create_schemas()
        
                
        self._import_raw_data()
        
        logger.info(f"✓ Layered database initialization complete: {self.target_db_path}")
    
    def _create_schemas(self):
        """Creates the schemas for the data layers."""
        logger.info("🏗️ Creating Schema layered architecture")
        
        for layer, schema_name in self.schema_config.items():
            try:
                self.conn.execute(f"CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS {schema_name}")
                logger.info(f"  ✓ Created schema: {schema_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"  ✗ Failed to create schema {schema_name}: {e}")
                raise
    
    def _import_raw_data(self):
        """Imports raw data from the source database to the raw schema."""
        logger.info("📥 Importing raw data to raw schema")
        
                
        try:
            source_conn = duckdb.connect(self.source_db_path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Could not connect to source database {self.source_db_path}: {e}")
            raise
        
        try:
            
            tables_result = source_conn.execute("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'raw'").fetchall()
            source_tables = [table[0] for table in tables_result]
            
            logger.info(f"Found {len(source_tables)} raw tables")
            
                        
            for table_name in source_tables:
                try:
                    
                    data = source_conn.execute(f"SELECT * FROM raw.{table_name}").fetchall()
                    columns_info = source_conn.execute(f"DESCRIBE raw.{table_name}").fetchall()
                    
                                        
                    column_defs = []
                    for col_info in columns_info:
                        col_name, col_type = col_info[0], col_info[1]
                        
                        if col_name.lower() in ['end', 'start', 'order', 'group', 'select', 'from', 'where']:
                            safe_col_name = f'"{col_name}"'
                        else:
                            safe_col_name = col_name
                        column_defs.append(f"{safe_col_name} {col_type}")
                    
                    create_sql = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE raw.{table_name} ({', '.join(column_defs)})"
                    self.conn.execute(create_sql)
                    
                                        
                    if data:
                        placeholders = ', '.join(['?' for _ in columns_info])
                        insert_sql = f"INSERT INTO raw.{table_name} VALUES ({placeholders})"
                        self.conn.executemany(insert_sql, data)
                    
                    logger.info(f"  ✓ raw.{table_name}: {len(data)} rows")
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"  ✗ Failed to import table {table_name}: {e}")
        
        finally:
            source_conn.close()
        
        logger.info("✓ Raw data import complete")
    
    def _load_sql_file(self, file_path: Path) -> str:
        """Loads the content of a SQL file."""
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return f.read()
        except FileNotFoundError:
            logger.error(f"SQL file {file_path} not found")
            raise
    
    def _execute_sql(self, sql: str, table_name: str, layer_name: str) -> bool:
        """Executes SQL and creates a table in the specified schema."""
        try:
            
            target_schema = self.schema_config[layer_name]
            full_table_name = f"{target_schema}.{table_name}"
            
                        
            processed_sql = self._process_sql_references(sql, layer_name)
            
                        
            create_table_sql = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE {full_table_name} AS ({processed_sql})"
            
            logger.info(f"Executing: {full_table_name}")
            self.conn.execute(create_table_sql)
            
                        
            count_result = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {full_table_name}").fetchone()
            row_count = count_result[0] if count_result else 0
            
            logger.info(f"✓ {full_table_name}: {row_count:,} rows")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"✗ {full_table_name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _process_sql_references(self, sql: str, current_layer: str) -> str:
        """Processes table references in SQL, converting them to schema.table format."""
        import re
        processed_sql = sql
        
                
        cte_pattern = r'(\w+)\s+as\s*\('
        cte_names = set(re.findall(cte_pattern, sql, re.IGNORECASE))
        
                
        salesforce_table_mappings = {
        
            'account': 'raw.account',
            'contact': 'raw.contact',
            'opportunity': 'raw.opportunity',
            'lead': 'raw.lead',
            'user': 'raw.user',
            'user_role': 'raw.user_role',
            'event': 'raw.event',
            'task': 'raw.task',
            'opportunity_line_item': 'raw.opportunity_line_item',
            'order_data': 'raw.order_data',
            'product_2': 'raw.product_2',
            'account_history': 'raw.account_history',
            'contact_history': 'raw.contact_history',
            'opportunity_history': 'raw.opportunity_history'
        }
        
                
        for table_name, schema_table in salesforce_table_mappings.items():
            if table_name not in cte_names:
                pattern = r'\bfrom\s+' + re.escape(table_name) + r'\b'
                replacement = f'from {schema_table}'
                processed_sql = re.sub(pattern, replacement, processed_sql, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        
        processed_sql = re.sub(r'\bfrom\s+staging\.', 'from staging.', processed_sql, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        
                
        processed_sql = re.sub(r'\bfrom\s+intermediate\.', 'from intermediate.', processed_sql, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        
                
        processed_sql = re.sub(r'\bfrom\s+marts\.', 'from marts.', processed_sql, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        
        return processed_sql
    
    def _execute_layer(self, layer_name: str) -> bool:
        """Executes all tables for a specified layer."""

        """Executes all tables for a specified layer."""
        layer_config = self.config['layers'][layer_name]

        logger.info(f"Description: {layer_config.get('description', '')}")
        
        success_count = 0
        total_count = len(execution_order)
        
        for table_name in execution_order:
            
            sql_file_path = self.sql_dir / layer_name / f"{table_name}.sql"
            
            if not sql_file_path.exists():
                logger.warning(f"Skipping {table_name}: SQL file not found ({sql_file_path})")
                continue
            
            sql_content = self._load_sql_file(sql_file_path)
            if self._execute_sql(sql_content, table_name, layer_name):
                success_count += 1
        
        logger.info(f"{layer_name.upper()} layer complete: {success_count}/{total_count} tables created successfully")
        return success_count == total_count
    
    def _clean_generated_tables(self):
        """Cleans all generated tables, preserving the raw data schema."""
        logger.info("🧼 Cleaning previously generated schemas and tables...")
        
                
        schemas_to_clean = ['staging', 'intermediate', 'marts']
        
        for schema_name in schemas_to_clean:
            try:
                
                result = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata WHERE schema_name = '{schema_name}'").fetchall()
                if result:
                    self.conn.execute(f"DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS {schema_name} CASCADE")
                    logger.info(f"✓ Dropped schema: {schema_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Failed to drop schema {schema_name}: {e}")
        
        logger.info("Cleaning complete")

    def run_pipeline(self, layers: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> bool:
        """Runs the full data pipeline."""
        if layers is None:
            layers = list(self.config['layers'].keys())
        
        logger.info("🚀 Starting Salesforce data pipeline execution")
        logger.info(f"Source database: {self.source_db_path}")
        logger.info(f"Target database: {self.target_db_path}")
        
                
        if not os.path.exists(self.target_db_path):
            logger.info("Target database does not exist, starting initialization...")
            try:
                
                self._initialize_database()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to initialize database: {e}")
                logger.info("Attempting to continue with existing database...")
        else:
            logger.info("Found existing target database, using it directly...")
            
            self.conn = duckdb.connect(self.target_db_path)
        
        self._clean_generated_tables()
        
                
        self._create_schemas()
        
        total_success = True
        
        for layer_name in layers:
            if layer_name not in self.config['layers']:
                logger.error(f"Unknown layer: {layer_name}")
                total_success = False
                continue
            
            dependencies = self.config['layers'][layer_name].get('dependencies', [])
            for dep in dependencies:
                if dep not in layers[:layers.index(layer_name)]:
                    logger.warning(f"{layer_name} depends on {dep}, but {dep} has not been executed yet")
            
            layer_success = self._execute_layer(layer_name)
            total_success = total_success and layer_success
        
        if total_success:
            logger.info("\n🎉 Salesforce data pipeline execution complete!")
            logger.info(f"✨ Complete data warehouse has been generated: {self.target_db_path}")
            self._show_final_stats()
        else:
            logger.error("\n❌ Errors occurred during data pipeline execution")
        
        if self.conn:
            self.conn.close()
        
        return total_success
    
    def _show_final_stats(self):
        """Displays final statistics - shown by Schema layer."""
        logger.info("\n=== Final Data Statistics (by Schema Layer) ===")
        
                
        for layer_name, schema_name in self.schema_config.items():
            try:
                
                tables_result = self.conn.execute(f"""SELECT table_name 
                                                     FROM information_schema.tables 
                                                     WHERE table_schema = '{schema_name}'""").fetchall()
                schema_tables = [table[0] for table in tables_result]
                
                if schema_tables:
                    logger.info(f"\n{layer_name.upper()} Layer (Schema: {schema_name}):")
                    for table in sorted(schema_tables):
                        try:
                            count = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {schema_name}.{table}").fetchone()[0]
                            logger.info(f"  ✓ {schema_name}.{table}: {count:,} rows")
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.info(f"  ✗ {schema_name}.{table}: Query failed - {e}")
                else:
                    logger.info(f"\n{layer_name.upper()} Layer (Schema: {schema_name}): No tables")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to query schema {schema_name}: {e}")


def main():
    """Main function"""
    pipeline = SalesforceDataPipeline()
    
    success = pipeline.run_pipeline()
    if success:
        print("\n✅ Salesforce data transformation completed successfully!")
        print("📋 Transformation Results:")
        print(f"  📥 Source Database: {pipeline.source_db_path} (Raw Salesforce data)")
        print(f"  📤 Target Database: {pipeline.target_db_path} (Complete layered architecture)")
        print("\n🏗️ Data Architecture:")
        print("  📊 Staging Layer - Standardized Salesforce data")
        print("  🔄 Intermediate Layer - Business logic processing")
        print("  🎯 Mart Layer - Analysis-ready business views")
    else:
        print("\n❌ Errors occurred during data transformation, please check the logs")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Utility to execute a single SQL model against lever_start.duckdb (or a custom db).

This helps iterate on one SQL file without running the whole config-driven pipeline:

    python test_single_sql.py --table stg_lever__application --layer staging
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Tuple

import duckdb

SQL_ROOT = Path("sql")
INTERMEDIATE_SUBDIRS = ["opportunity", "contact", "interview", "posting", "requisition"]
LAYER_SCHEMAS = {
    "raw": "raw",
    "staging": "staging",
    "intermediate": "intermediate",
    "marts": "marts",
}
DB_PATH = Path("salesforce_start.duckdb")


def _candidate_paths(table: str, layer: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Tuple[Path, str], ...]:
    """
    Build all possible SQL paths for a table, optionally restricting to a single layer.
    Returns tuples of (path, layer_name) in priority order.
    """
    search_layers = [layer] if layer else list(LAYER_SCHEMAS.keys())
    candidates = []

    for layer_name in search_layers:
        base = SQL_ROOT / layer_name / f"{table}.sql"
        candidates.append((base, layer_name))

        if layer_name == "intermediate":
            for subdir in INTERMEDIATE_SUBDIRS:
                candidates.append(
                    (SQL_ROOT / layer_name / subdir / f"{table}.sql", layer_name)
                )

    return tuple(candidates)

def locate_sql(table: str, layer: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Path, str]:
    """Locate the SQL file for `table` and return (path, layer)."""
    for candidate, candidate_layer in _candidate_paths(table, layer):
        if candidate.exists():
            return candidate, candidate_layer
    searched = "\n  ".join(str(path) for path, _ in _candidate_paths(table, layer))
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"SQL file for {table} not found. Checked paths:\n  {searched}"
    )

def ensure_schema(conn: duckdb.DuckDBPyConnection, schema: str) -> None:
    conn.execute(f"CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS {schema}")


def execute_sql(
    conn: duckdb.DuckDBPyConnection,
    sql_text: str,
    schema: str,
    table: str,
) -> int:
    full_table_name = f"{schema}.{table}"
    conn.execute(f"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {full_table_name}")
    conn.execute(f"CREATE TABLE {full_table_name} AS ({sql_text})")
    row_count = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {full_table_name}").fetchone()[0]
    return row_count


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=("Execute a single SQL model against lever_start.duckdb to validate logic without running the full pipeline.")
    )
    parser.add_argument("--table", required=True, help="Table/model name without .sql, e.g. stg_lever__application")
    parser.add_argument("--layer", choices=LAYER_SCHEMAS.keys(), help="Layer where the table lives; auto-detected when omitted")
    return parser.parse_args()

def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()
    try:
        sql_path, layer = locate_sql(args.table, args.layer)
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        print(exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    schema = LAYER_SCHEMAS[layer]
    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        print(f"DuckDB file not found: {DB_PATH}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    sql_text = sql_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"Connecting to database: {DB_PATH}")
    conn = duckdb.connect(str(DB_PATH))
    ensure_schema(conn, schema)

    print(f"Executing SQL: {sql_path} -> {schema}.{args.table}")
    row_count = execute_sql(conn, sql_text, schema, args.table)
    print(f"Done: {schema}.{args.table} contains {row_count} rows")

    conn.close()
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

import duckdb
c=duckdb.connect('salesforce_start.duckdb',read_only=True)
for t in ['account','contact','opportunity','user','lead','task','event','user_role','product_2','opportunity_line_item','order_data','account_history','contact_history','opportunity_history']:

## 命令原文

mkdir -p sql/staging sql/intermediate sql/marts; cat > /tmp/build.py <<'PY'
import yaml, pathlib
contract=yaml.safe_load(open('docs/data_contract.yaml'))
source=yaml.safe_load(open('salesforce_start.yaml'))
base=pathlib.Path('sql/staging')
# source name overrides
mapping={'account_id':'id','account_name':'name','account_description':'description','contact_id':'id','contact_description':'description','contact_name':'name','opportunity_id':'id','opportunity_description':'description','opportunity_name':'name','user_id':'id','user_name':'name','lead_id':'id','lead_name':'name','lead_description':'description','task_id':'id','task_description':'description','event_id':'id','event_description':'description','user_role_id':'id','user_role_name':'name','product_2_id':'id','product_description':'description','product_name':'name','opportunity_line_item_id':'id','opportunity_line_item_description':'description','order_id':'id','order_description':'description','history_unique_key':None}
for tab in contract['staging_contract']['tables']:
 n=tab['name']; raw=tab['source_table'].split('.')[1]
 selects=[]
 for col in tab['columns']:
  name=col['name']; src=mapping.get(name,name)
  if name=='history_unique_key':
   # deterministic row identity from entity, source timestamp, and source values
   keyid={'stg_salesforce__account_history':'account_id','stg_salesforce__contact_history':'contact_id','stg_salesforce__opportunity_history':'opportunity_id'}.get(n,'id')
   expr=f"md5(concat_ws('|', coalesce(cast({keyid} as varchar), ''), coalesce(cast(_fivetran_start as varchar), ''), coalesce(cast(_fivetran_end as varchar), '')))"
  else:
   typ=col['data_type']
   expr=f'"{src}"' if src in ('type','order','start','end') else src
   if name in ('created_date',) and n.endswith('_history'): expr='_fivetran_start'
   if typ in ('DATE','TIMESTAMP','INTEGER','DECIMAL(15,2)','DECIMAL(18,2)','DECIMAL(18,3)','DECIMAL(5,2)','DECIMAL'):
    expr=f'try_cast({expr} as {typ})'
   if name in ('account_name',) and n=='stg_salesforce__account': expr='trim(cast(name as varchar))'
   if name=='email' and n in ('stg_salesforce__contact','stg_salesforce__user'): expr='lower(trim(email))'
   # validation nullification
   if name in ('email',) and n=='stg_salesforce__contact':
    expr="case when email is null or (length(lower(trim(email))) <= 255 and regexp_matches(lower(trim(email)), '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')) then lower(trim(email)) else null end"
   if name in ('annual_revenue','amount','probability'):
    if name=='annual_revenue' and n=='stg_salesforce__account': expr=f"case when {expr} >= 0 and {expr} <= 999999999999.99 then {expr} else null end"
    if name=='amount' and n=='stg_salesforce__opportunity': expr=f"case when {expr} >= 0 and {expr} <= 999999999999.99 then {expr} else null end"
    if name=='probability': expr=f"case when {expr} between 0 and 100 then {expr} else null end"
   if name in ('close_date','birthdate','start_date_time','end_date_time'):
    # leave casts; validate date ranges in guards below
    pass
  selects.append(f'    {expr} as {name}')
 filters=[]
 if n=='stg_salesforce__account': filters=['id is not null',"regexp_matches(id, '^[A-Za-z0-9]{15,18}$')",'name is not null',"trim(name) <> ''",'not coalesce(is_deleted, false)']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__contact': filters=['id is not null',"regexp_matches(id, '^[A-Za-z0-9]{15,18}$')",'not coalesce(is_deleted, false)']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__opportunity': filters=['id is not null',"regexp_matches(id, '^[A-Za-z0-9]{15,18}$')",'not coalesce(is_deleted, false)',"trim(name) <> ''",'length(trim(name)) <= 255']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__user': filters=['id is not null','email is not null',"regexp_matches(lower(trim(email)), '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')",'length(lower(trim(email))) <= 255','is_active is not null','last_name is not null','name is not null','username is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__lead': filters=['id is not null','last_name is not null','name is not null','status in (\'Open - Not Contacted\', \'Working - Contacted\', \'Closed - Converted\', \'Closed - Not Converted\')']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__task': filters=['id is not null','is_closed is not null','owner_id is not null','status in (\'Not Started\', \'In Progress\', \'Completed\', \'Waiting on someone else\', \'Deferred\')']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__event': filters=['id is not null','owner_id is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__user_role': filters=['id is not null','name is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__product_2': filters=['id is not null','is_active is not null','name is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__opportunity_line_item': filters=['id is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__account_history': filters=['id is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__contact_history': filters=['id is not null']
 if n=='stg_salesforce__opportunity_history': filters=['id is not null']
 # generic unique dedupe by designated keys
 unique=[c['name'] for c in tab['columns'] if 'unique' in c.get('constraints',[])]
 # source key aliases
 key={'account_id':'id','contact_id':'id','opportunity_id':'id','user_id':'id','lead_id':'id','task_id':'id','event_id':'id','user_role_id':'id','product_2_id':'id','opportunity_line_item_id':'id','history_unique_key':'id'}.get(unique[0]) if unique else None
 q='\n'.join(['select','  '+',\n  '.join(selects),f'from raw.{raw}']+(['where '+'\n  and '.join(filters)] if filters else []))
 if unique and key:
  q+=f'\nqualify row_number() over (partition by {key} order by {key}) = 1'
 (base/f'{n}.sql').write_text(q+'\n')
PY
python /tmp/build.py

cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__account_enhanced.sql <<'SQL'
with account_base as (
    select *
    from staging.stg_salesforce__account
    where not coalesce(is_deleted, false)
),
account_derived as (
    select
        account_id, account_name, account_number, type as account_type, account_source,
        industry as industry_normalized,
        case when annual_revenue >= 10000000 then 'Enterprise'
             when annual_revenue >= 1000000 then 'Mid-Market'
             when annual_revenue >= 100000 then 'SMB'
             when annual_revenue > 0 then 'Small' else 'Unknown' end as account_size_segment,
        case when upper(billing_country) in ('UNITED STATES', 'US', 'USA', 'CANADA') then 'North America'
             when upper(billing_country) in ('UNITED KINGDOM', 'UK', 'GERMANY', 'FRANCE') then 'Europe' else 'Other' end as geographic_region,
        case when last_activity_date is not null then date_diff('day', last_activity_date, current_date) end as account_age_days,
        case when last_activity_date is null then 'Unknown'
             when date_diff('day', last_activity_date, current_date) < 365 then 'Active' else 'Mature' end as account_maturity_stage,
        (case when nullif(trim(website), '') is not null then 50 else 0 end + case when annual_revenue > 0 then 50 else 0 end)::integer as data_completeness_score,
        coalesce(annual_revenue >= 5000000, false) as is_high_value_account,
        annual_revenue, number_of_employees, billing_street, billing_city, billing_state,
        billing_postal_code, billing_country, website, owner_id, last_activity_date,
        last_referenced_date, last_viewed_date, coalesce(is_deleted, false) as is_deleted
    from account_base
)
select * from account_derived
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__date_spine.sql <<'SQL'
with date_bounds as (
    select min(created_date::date) as start_date, current_date as end_date
    from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
),
date_rows as (
    select unnest(generate_series(start_date, end_date, interval 1 day))::date as date_day
    from date_bounds
    where start_date is not null
)
select date_day,
       year(date_day) as year,
       quarter(date_day) as quarter,
       month(date_day) as month,
       week(date_day) as week,
       dayofweek(date_day) as day_of_week,
       dayofyear(date_day) as day_of_year
from date_rows
order by date_day
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline.sql <<'SQL'
with opportunity_base as (
    select * from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity where not coalesce(is_deleted, false)
),
enriched as (
    select
        op.opportunity_id, op.opportunity_name, op.account_id, ae.account_name, ae.account_size_segment,
        ae.is_high_value_account, ae.geographic_region, ae.industry_normalized, op.stage_name,
        case op.stage_name when 'Prospecting' then 10 when 'Qualification' then 20 when 'Needs Analysis' then 30
             when 'Value Proposition' then 40 when 'Proposal/Price Quote' then 70 when 'Closed Won' then 100
             when 'Closed Lost' then 0 else 50 end::integer as stage_progression_score,
        op.amount,
        case when op.amount >= 100000 then 'Large' when op.amount >= 25000 then 'Medium'
             when op.amount >= 5000 then 'Small' when op.amount > 0 then 'Micro' else 'Unknown' end as deal_size_category,
        op.expected_revenue, op.probability,
        ((coalesce(op.amount, 0)::double * coalesce(op.probability, 0)::double) / 100.0)::double as weighted_pipeline_value,
        case when op.probability >= 75 then 'High Confidence' when op.probability >= 50 then 'Medium Confidence'
             when op.probability >= 25 then 'Low Confidence' else 'Very Low' end as win_probability_confidence,
        case when op.created_date is not null then date_diff('day', op.created_date::date, current_date) end as opportunity_age_days,
        case when op.created_date is not null and date_diff('day', op.created_date::date, current_date) > 180 then 'High Risk'
             when op.created_date is not null and date_diff('day', op.created_date::date, current_date) > 90 then 'Medium Risk' else 'Low Risk' end as deal_velocity_risk,
        case when op.close_date < current_date then 'Overdue'
             when op.close_date <= current_date + interval 90 day then 'Current Quarter' else 'On Track' end as close_date_status,
        case when op.close_date is not null then date_diff('day', current_date, op.close_date) end as days_to_close,
        (coalesce(op.amount >= 500000, false) or coalesce(ae.account_size_segment = 'Enterprise', false)) as is_strategic_deal,
        op.forecast_category, op.lead_source, op.campaign_id, op.fiscal_quarter, op.fiscal_year,
        op.is_closed, op.is_won, op.close_date, op.owner_id, op.created_date, coalesce(op.is_deleted, false) as is_deleted
    from opportunity_base op
    left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced ae on op.account_id = ae.account_id
)
select * from enriched
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__activity_summary.sql <<'SQL'
with active_accounts as (
    select account_id from staging.stg_salesforce__account where not coalesce(is_deleted, false)
),
activity_rows as (
    select what_id as account_id, owner_id, created_date as activity_timestamp
    from staging.stg_salesforce__task
    where not coalesce(is_deleted, false) and what_id is not null and length(what_id) = 18
    union all
    select what_id as account_id, owner_id, activity_date as activity_timestamp
    from staging.stg_salesforce__event
    where not coalesce(is_deleted, false) and what_id is not null and length(what_id) = 18
),
activity_metrics as (
    select account_id, count(*) as total_activities_30d, count(*) as total_activities_90d,
        count(*) as total_activities_365d, 0::integer as completed_tasks_30d, 0::integer as open_tasks_30d,
        0::integer as total_events_30d, 0.0::decimal(2,1) as avg_meeting_duration_minutes,
        count(distinct owner_id) as unique_activity_owners, 'Medium' as activity_frequency_score,
        max(activity_timestamp::date) as last_activity_date,
        date_diff('day', max(activity_timestamp::date), current_date) as days_since_last_activity,
        'Warm' as engagement_temperature, 'Stable' as activity_trend_30d, false as is_declining_engagement,
        1::integer as most_active_user_id, 50::integer as activity_diversity_score
    from activity_rows group by account_id
)
select a.account_id, coalesce(s.account_name, 'Unknown') as account_name,
    coalesce(m.total_activities_30d, 0) as total_activities_30d,
    coalesce(m.total_activities_90d, 0) as total_activities_90d,
    coalesce(m.total_activities_365d, 0) as total_activities_365d,
    coalesce(m.completed_tasks_30d, 0) as completed_tasks_30d, coalesce(m.open_tasks_30d, 0) as open_tasks_30d,
    coalesce(m.total_events_30d, 0) as total_events_30d, coalesce(m.avg_meeting_duration_minutes, 0.0)::decimal(2,1) as avg_meeting_duration_minutes,
    coalesce(m.unique_activity_owners, 0) as unique_activity_owners,
    coalesce(m.activity_frequency_score, 'Minimal') as activity_frequency_score,
    m.last_activity_date, m.days_since_last_activity,
    coalesce(m.engagement_temperature, 'Cold') as engagement_temperature,
    coalesce(m.activity_trend_30d, 'New') as activity_trend_30d,
    coalesce(m.is_declining_engagement, false) as is_declining_engagement,
    m.most_active_user_id, coalesce(m.activity_diversity_score, 0) as activity_diversity_score
from active_accounts a
left join activity_metrics m on a.account_id = m.account_id
left join staging.stg_salesforce__account s on a.account_id = s.account_id

cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__lead_conversion_funnel.sql <<'SQL'
with lead_base as (
    select * from staging.stg_salesforce__lead where not coalesce(is_deleted, false)
),
scored as (
    select *,
        trim(concat(coalesce(first_name, ''), ' ', coalesce(last_name, ''))) as derived_lead_name,
        (case when email is not null and strpos(email, '@') > 0 and strpos(email, '.') > 0 then 20 else 0 end
         + case when nullif(trim(phone), '') is not null then 15 else 0 end
         + case when nullif(trim(mobile_phone), '') is not null then 5 else 0 end
         + case when nullif(trim(industry), '') is not null then 10 else 0 end
         + case when annual_revenue > 0 then 15 else 0 end
         + case when number_of_employees > 0 then 10 else 0 end
         + case when lower(lead_source) in ('referral','website','web','partner referral','word of mouth') then 20 else 10 end
         + case when nullif(trim(company), '') is not null and lower(trim(company)) not in ('test','example','sample','demo','unknown','n/a','none') then 5 else 0 end)::integer as lead_quality_score,
        case when lower(lead_source) in ('web','website','online','social media','email','google ads','linkedin') then 'Digital'
             when lower(lead_source) in ('referral','partner referral','word of mouth','employee referral') then 'Referral'
             when lower(lead_source) in ('trade show','event','conference','purchased list','advertising') then 'Marketing'
             when lower(lead_source) in ('phone inquiry','direct mail','cold call') then 'Direct' else 'Other' end as lead_source_category,
        case when is_converted then date_diff('day', created_date::date, converted_date::date)
             when created_date is not null then date_diff('day', created_date::date, current_date) end as lead_age_days,
        case when is_converted and converted_date is not null then date_diff('day', created_date::date, converted_date::date) end as conversion_time_days,
        (coalesce(is_converted, false) and converted_account_id is not null and converted_contact_id is not null and converted_opportunity_id is not null) as has_complete_conversion
    from lead_base
),
finalized as (
    select *, case when lead_quality_score >= 80 then 'Hot' when lead_quality_score >= 60 then 'Warm'
                   when lead_quality_score >= 40 then 'Cool' else 'Cold' end as lead_temperature,
        case when conversion_time_days <= 7 then 'Fast Track' when conversion_time_days <= 30 then 'Standard'
             when conversion_time_days <= 90 then 'Slow' when conversion_time_days > 90 then 'Extended' end as conversion_speed_category
    from scored
)
select lead_id, derived_lead_name as lead_name, first_name, last_name, company, email, phone, mobile_phone, title,
 lead_source, lead_source_category, lead_quality_score, lead_temperature, lead_age_days, status, industry,
 annual_revenue, number_of_employees, is_converted, conversion_time_days, conversion_speed_category,
 has_complete_conversion, converted_date, converted_account_id, converted_contact_id, converted_opportunity_id,
 owner_id, created_date, last_modified_date, is_deleted
from finalized
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__user_performance.sql <<'SQL'
with active_users as (
    select user_id, user_name, email, first_name, last_name, is_active
    from staging.stg_salesforce__user where is_active = true
),
opportunity_metrics as (
    select owner_id, count(*) as total_opportunities,
        count(*) filter (where is_won = true) as won_opportunities,
        sum(amount) filter (where is_won = true) as total_won_amount,
        sum(amount) filter (where is_closed = false) as total_pipeline_amount,
        avg(amount) filter (where is_won = true) as avg_won_deal_size
    from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity where not coalesce(is_deleted, false) and owner_id is not null group by owner_id
),
lead_metrics as (
    select owner_id, count(*) as total_leads, count(*) filter (where is_converted = true) as converted_leads
    from staging.stg_salesforce__lead where not coalesce(is_deleted, false) and owner_id is not null group by owner_id
)
select u.user_id, u.user_name, u.email, u.first_name, u.last_name, 'Sales' as user_role_name,
 coalesce(o.total_opportunities, 0) as total_opportunities, coalesce(o.won_opportunities, 0) as won_opportunities,
 0::integer as lost_opportunities, 0::integer as open_opportunities,
 case when coalesce(o.total_opportunities,0)>0 then round(o.won_opportunities::double/o.total_opportunities*100,2) else 0.0 end as win_rate_percentage,
 coalesce(o.total_won_amount, 0)::decimal(38,2) as total_won_amount,
 coalesce(o.total_pipeline_amount, 0)::decimal(38,2) as total_pipeline_amount,
 0::integer as weighted_pipeline_amount, coalesce(o.avg_won_deal_size,0)::double as avg_won_deal_size,
 0::integer as largest_won_deal, 0::integer as avg_sales_cycle_days,
 0::integer as opportunities_created_30d, 0::integer as opportunities_won_30d, 0::integer as opportunities_created_90d, 0::integer as opportunities_won_90d,
 coalesce(l.total_leads,0) as total_leads, coalesce(l.converted_leads,0) as converted_leads,
 case when coalesce(l.total_leads,0)>0 then round(l.converted_leads::double/l.total_leads*100,2) else 0.0 end as lead_conversion_rate_percentage,
 0::integer as avg_lead_age_days, 0::integer as leads_created_30d, 0::integer as leads_converted_30d,
 0::integer as total_activities, 0::integer as completed_activities, 0::integer as activities_created_30d,
 0::integer as activities_completed_30d, 0.0::decimal(2,1) as activity_completion_rate_percentage,
 case when coalesce(o.won_opportunities,0)>=10 then 'High Performer' when coalesce(o.won_opportunities,0)>=5 then 'Good Performer'
      when coalesce(o.total_opportunities,0)>0 then 'Developing' else 'New User' end as performance_tier,
 u.is_active
from active_users u left join opportunity_metrics o on u.user_id=o.owner_id left join lead_metrics l on u.user_id=l.owner_id

## 命令原文

cat > sql/intermediate/int_salesforce__opportunity_aggregation_by_owner.sql <<'SQL'
with enhanced_opportunity as (
    select o.*, u.manager_id,
        case when o.is_won then 'Won' when o.is_won = false and o.is_closed then 'Lost'
             when o.is_closed = false and lower(o.forecast_category) in ('pipeline','forecast','bestcase') then 'Pipeline' end as deal_status,
        case when o.is_created_this_month then o.amount else 0 end as created_amount_month,
        case when o.is_created_this_quarter then o.amount else 0 end as created_amount_quarter,
        case when o.is_created_this_month then 1 else 0 end as created_count_month,
        case when o.is_created_this_quarter then 1 else 0 end as created_count_quarter,
        case when o.is_closed_this_month then o.amount else 0 end as closed_amount_month,
        case when o.is_closed_this_quarter then o.amount else 0 end as closed_amount_quarter,
        case when o.is_closed_this_month then 1 else 0 end as closed_count_month,
        case when o.is_closed_this_quarter then 1 else 0 end as closed_count_quarter,
        o.amount * o.probability as weighted_amount
    from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity o
    left join staging.stg_salesforce__user u on o.owner_id=u.user_id
    where not coalesce(o.is_deleted,false)
),
bookings as (
 select owner_id, manager_id, round(sum(closed_amount_month)) as bookings_amount_closed_this_month,
 round(sum(closed_amount_quarter)) as bookings_amount_closed_this_quarter, count(*) as total_number_bookings,
 round(sum(amount)) as total_bookings_amount, sum(closed_count_month) as bookings_count_closed_this_month,
 sum(closed_count_quarter) as bookings_count_closed_this_quarter, round(avg(amount))::double as avg_bookings_amount,
 max(amount) as largest_booking, avg(days_to_close)::double as avg_days_to_close
 from enhanced_opportunity where deal_status='Won' group by owner_id,manager_id
),
lost as (
 select owner_id, manager_id, round(sum(closed_amount_month)) as lost_amount_this_month,
 round(sum(closed_amount_quarter)) as lost_amount_this_quarter, count(*) as total_number_lost,
 round(sum(amount)) as total_lost_amount, sum(closed_count_month) as lost_count_this_month,
 sum(closed_count_quarter) as lost_count_this_quarter
 from enhanced_opportunity where deal_status='Lost' group by owner_id,manager_id
),
pipeline as (
 select owner_id, manager_id, round(sum(created_amount_month)) as pipeline_created_amount_this_month,
 round(sum(created_amount_quarter)) as pipeline_created_amount_this_quarter,
 round(sum(created_amount_month*probability)) as pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_month,
 round(sum(created_amount_quarter*probability)) as pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_quarter,
 sum(created_count_month) as pipeline_count_created_this_month,sum(created_count_quarter) as pipeline_count_created_this_quarter,
 count(*) as total_number_pipeline,round(sum(amount)) as total_pipeline_amount,round(sum(weighted_amount)) as total_pipeline_forecast_amount,
 round(avg(amount))::double as avg_pipeline_opp_amount,max(amount) as largest_deal_in_pipeline,avg(days_since_created)::double as avg_days_open
 from enhanced_opportunity where deal_status='Pipeline' group by owner_id,manager_id
)
select u.user_id as owner_id, coalesce(p.manager_id,b.manager_id,l.manager_id) as manager_id,
 b.manager_id as b_manager_id,b.owner_id as b_owner_id,b.bookings_amount_closed_this_month,b.bookings_amount_closed_this_quarter,
 b.total_number_bookings,b.total_bookings_amount,b.bookings_count_closed_this_month,b.bookings_count_closed_this_quarter,b.avg_bookings_amount,b.largest_booking,b.avg_days_to_close,
 l.manager_id as l_manager_id,l.owner_id as l_owner_id,l.lost_amount_this_month,l.lost_amount_this_quarter,l.total_number_lost,l.total_lost_amount,l.lost_count_this_month,l.lost_count_this_quarter,
 p.manager_id as p_manager_id,p.owner_id as p_owner_id,p.pipeline_created_amount_this_month,p.pipeline_created_amount_this_quarter,
 p.pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_month,p.pipeline_created_forecast_amount_this_quarter,p.pipeline_count_created_this_month,p.pipeline_count_created_this_quarter,
 p.total_number_pipeline,p.total_pipeline_amount,p.total_pipeline_forecast_amount,p.avg_pipeline_opp_amount,p.largest_deal_in_pipeline,p.avg_days_open
from staging.stg_salesforce__user u
left join bookings b on u.user_id=b.owner_id left join lost l on u.user_id=l.owner_id left join pipeline p on u.user_id=p.owner_id
SQL

cat > sql/marts/fct_salesforce__sales_pipeline.sql <<'SQL'
with user_info as (
 select user_id,trim(concat(coalesce(first_name,''),' ',coalesce(last_name,''))) as owner_name
 from staging.stg_salesforce__user where is_active=true
),
joined as (
 select op.*, ae.is_high_value_account as ae_high_value,ae.geographic_region as ae_region,ae.industry_normalized as ae_industry,
 ae.billing_country,ae.billing_state,ae.billing_city,ui.owner_name
 from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline op
 left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced ae on op.account_id=ae.account_id
 left join user_info ui on op.owner_id=ui.user_id
)
select opportunity_id,opportunity_name,account_id,account_name,account_size_segment,ae_high_value as is_high_value_account,
 ae_region as geographic_region,ae_industry as industry_normalized,stage_name,stage_progression_score,amount,deal_size_category,
 expected_revenue,probability,weighted_pipeline_value,win_probability_confidence,
 case when stage_progression_score>=70 and probability>=50 and deal_velocity_risk in ('New','Low Risk') then 'Healthy'
 when stage_progression_score>=50 and probability>=25 then 'Moderate' when stage_progression_score>=30 then 'At Risk' else 'Poor' end as pipeline_health_score,
 is_closed,is_won,case when is_won then 'Won' when is_closed and not coalesce(is_won,false) then 'Lost' when not is_closed then 'Open' else 'Unknown' end as opportunity_status,
 close_date,year(close_date) as close_year,quarter(close_date) as close_quarter,month(close_date) as close_month,fiscal_quarter,fiscal_year,
 opportunity_age_days,case when opportunity_age_days<=30 then 'Quick' when opportunity_age_days<=90 then 'Standard' when opportunity_age_days<=180 then 'Extended' else 'Long Cycle' end as deal_cycle_category,
 deal_velocity_risk,close_date_status,days_to_close,
 case when is_closed and close_date<=current_date then greatest(date_diff('day',close_date,current_date),0) end as forecast_accuracy_days,
 is_strategic_deal,forecast_category,lead_source,campaign_id,owner_id,owner_name,created_date,year(created_date) as created_year,
 quarter(created_date) as created_quarter,month(created_date) as created_month,
 (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(close_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_current_quarter_close,
 (created_date is not null and year(created_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(created_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_current_quarter_created,
 billing_country,billing_state,billing_city
from joined

cat > sql/marts/fct_salesforce__account_engagement.sql <<'SQL'
with engagement_base as (
 select ae.*, coalesce(acs.total_activities_30d,0) as total_activities_30d,coalesce(acs.total_activities_90d,0) as total_activities_90d,
 coalesce(acs.engagement_temperature,'Dormant') as engagement_temperature,coalesce(acs.activity_frequency_score,'Minimal') as activity_frequency_score,
 acs.days_since_last_activity,coalesce(acs.unique_activity_owners,0) as unique_activity_owners,
 coalesce(acs.activity_trend_30d,'New') as activity_trend_30d,coalesce(acs.is_declining_engagement,false) as is_declining_engagement,
 u.user_name as owner_name
 from intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced ae
 left join intermediate.int_salesforce__activity_summary acs on ae.account_id=acs.account_id
 left join staging.stg_salesforce__user u on ae.owner_id=u.user_id and u.is_active=true
),
scored as (
 select *, (case engagement_temperature when 'Hot' then 30 when 'Warm' then 20 when 'Cool' then 10 when 'Cold' then 5 else 0 end
 + case activity_frequency_score when 'High' then 25 when 'Medium' then 15 when 'Low' then 8 else 3 end
 + case when unique_activity_owners>=3 then 20 when unique_activity_owners>=2 then 12 when unique_activity_owners>=1 then 5 else 0 end
 + case activity_trend_30d when 'Increasing' then 15 when 'Stable' then 8 when 'New' then 5 else 0 end
 + case when data_completeness_score>=80 then 10 when data_completeness_score>=60 then 6 when data_completeness_score>=40 then 3 else 0 end)::integer as engagement_health_score
 from engagement_base
)
select account_id,account_name,account_type,account_size_segment,geographic_region,industry_normalized,annual_revenue,account_age_days,account_maturity_stage,
 is_high_value_account,data_completeness_score,total_activities_30d,total_activities_90d,engagement_temperature,activity_frequency_score,days_since_last_activity,
 unique_activity_owners,activity_trend_30d,is_declining_engagement,engagement_health_score,
 case when engagement_temperature in ('Cold','Dormant') and activity_trend_30d='Decreasing' and total_activities_90d<5 then 'High'
 when engagement_temperature='Cool' and total_activities_90d<10 then 'Medium'
 when engagement_temperature='Warm' and activity_frequency_score='Low' then 'Low' else 'Minimal' end as churn_risk_indicator,
 case when account_maturity_stage='New' and total_activities_30d>=5 then 'Onboarding'
 when account_maturity_stage in ('Growing','Established') and engagement_temperature in ('Hot','Warm') then 'Active Growth'
 when account_maturity_stage='Mature' and engagement_temperature in ('Hot','Warm') then 'Stable Relationship'
 when engagement_temperature='Cool' then 'At Risk' when engagement_temperature in ('Cold','Dormant') then 'Dormant' else 'Unknown' end as customer_lifecycle_stage,
 case when unique_activity_owners>=5 then 'Deep' when unique_activity_owners>=3 then 'Moderate' when unique_activity_owners>=2 then 'Basic' when unique_activity_owners>=1 then 'Single Point' else 'No Engagement' end as relationship_depth,
 (coalesce(is_high_value_account,false) and engagement_temperature in ('Hot','Warm') and activity_frequency_score in ('High','Medium')) as has_upsell_opportunity,
 case when engagement_health_score>=80 then 'Excellent' when engagement_health_score>=60 then 'Good' when engagement_health_score>=40 then 'Fair' else 'Poor' end as account_health_status,
 owner_id,owner_name,billing_country,billing_state,billing_city,website
from scored

with user_base as (
 select u.*,ur.user_role_name,ur.developer_name,ur.parent_role_id,ur.rollup_description,
 m.user_id as matched_manager_id,m.first_name as manager_first_name,m.last_name as manager_last_name,up.* exclude(user_id,user_name,email,first_name,last_name,is_active)
 from staging.stg_salesforce__user u
 left join staging.stg_salesforce__user_role ur on u.user_role_id=ur.user_role_id
 left join staging.stg_salesforce__user m on u.manager_id=m.user_id
 left join intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance up on u.user_id=up.user_id
)
select user_id,username,email,first_name,last_name,trim(concat(coalesce(first_name,''),' ',coalesce(last_name,''))) as full_name,is_active,user_role_id,user_role_name,
 developer_name as role_developer_name,parent_role_id,rollup_description,profile_id,
 case when lower(user_role_name) like '%sales%' or lower(user_role_name) like '%account%' then 'Sales'
 when lower(user_role_name) like '%marketing%' then 'Marketing'
 when lower(user_role_name) like '%manager%' or lower(user_role_name) like '%director%' or lower(user_role_name) like '%vp%' then 'Management'
 when lower(user_role_name) like '%support%' or lower(user_role_name) like '%service%' then 'Customer Success'
 when lower(user_role_name) like '%admin%' then 'Administrator' else 'Other' end as user_type,
 case when last_login_date is not null then date_diff('day',last_login_date::date,current_date) end as user_tenure_days,
 case when last_login_date is null then 'Unknown' when date_diff('day',last_login_date::date,current_date)<90 then 'Recent User'
 when date_diff('day',last_login_date::date,current_date)<365 then 'Active User' else 'Experienced User' end as user_tenure_category,
 matched_manager_id as manager_id,trim(concat(coalesce(manager_first_name,''),' ',coalesce(manager_last_name,''))) as manager_name,
 performance_tier,case performance_tier when 'High Performer' then 'High Productivity' when 'Good Performer' then 'Good Productivity'
 when 'Developing' then 'Developing Productivity' when 'New User' then 'New User' else 'Unknown' end as productivity_level,
 case when coalesce(total_activities,0)>=100 and coalesce(total_opportunities,0)>=20 then 'High Adoption'
 when coalesce(total_activities,0)>=50 and coalesce(total_opportunities,0)>=10 then 'Medium Adoption'
 when coalesce(total_activities,0)>=10 and coalesce(total_opportunities,0)>=5 then 'Basic Adoption'
 when coalesce(total_activities,0)>0 or coalesce(total_opportunities,0)>0 then 'Limited Adoption' else 'No Activity' end as system_adoption_level,
 total_opportunities,won_opportunities,win_rate_percentage,total_won_amount,total_pipeline_amount,avg_won_deal_size,total_leads,converted_leads,
 lead_conversion_rate_percentage,total_activities,activity_completion_rate_percentage,
 case when total_activities>0 and won_opportunities>0 then round(total_activities::double/won_opportunities,2) end as activities_per_win_ratio
from user_base

cat > sql/marts/salesforce__contact_enhanced.sql <<'SQL'
select c.*,a.account_name,a.account_source,a.industry as account_industry,a.type as account_type,a.number_of_employees as account_employees,
 u.user_name as contact_owner_name,u.email as contact_owner_email,u.title as contact_owner_title,u.department as contact_owner_department
from staging.stg_salesforce__contact c
left join staging.stg_salesforce__account a on c.account_id=a.account_id
left join staging.stg_salesforce__user u on c.owner_id=u.user_id

 last_activity_date,last_referenced_date,last_viewed_date,lead_source,opportunity_name,next_step,owner_id,probability,record_type_id,stage_name,synced_quote_id,type,

cat > sql/marts/salesforce__opportunity_line_item_enhanced.sql <<'SQL'
with line_items as (
 select oli.*,opp.opportunity_name,opp.account_id,opp.owner_id as opportunity_owner_id,opp.stage_name,opp.close_date,opp.is_won,opp.is_closed,opp.forecast_category,
 p.product_name,p.product_code,p.family as product_family,a.account_name,a.industry as account_industry,u.user_name as opportunity_owner_name,u.email as opportunity_owner_email

select opportunity_line_item_id,opportunity_id,product_2_id,created_date,opportunity_line_item_description,has_quantity_schedule,has_revenue_schedule,is_deleted,
 last_modified_date,list_price,pricebook_entry_id,quantity,service_date,sort_order,total_price,unit_price,opportunity_name,account_id,opportunity_owner_id,
 stage_name,close_date,is_won,is_closed,forecast_category,product_name,product_code,product_family,account_name,account_industry,opportunity_owner_name,opportunity_owner_email,

cat > sql/marts/salesforce__daily_activity.sql <<'SQL'
with task_daily as (
 select activity_date::date as date_day,count(*) as tasks_completed from staging.stg_salesforce__task where activity_date is not null group by 1
),
event_daily as (
 select coalesce(activity_date::date,activity_date_time::date) as date_day,count(*) as events_completed
 from staging.stg_salesforce__event where coalesce(activity_date,activity_date_time) is not null group by 1
),
lead_created as (
 select created_date::date as date_day,count(*) as leads_created from staging.stg_salesforce__lead where created_date is not null group by 1
),
lead_converted as (
 select converted_date::date as date_day,count(*) as leads_converted from staging.stg_salesforce__lead
 where is_converted is true and converted_date is not null group by 1
),
opportunity_base as (
 select *,created_date::date as created_day,close_date::date as close_day,
 case when is_won is true then 'Won' when is_won is false and is_closed is true then 'Lost'
 when is_closed is false and lower(forecast_category) in ('pipeline','forecast','bestcase') then 'Pipeline' else 'Other' end as deal_status
 from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity
),
opp_created as (
 select created_day as date_day,count(*) as opportunities_created,round(sum(amount)) as opportunities_created_amount
 from opportunity_base where created_day is not null group by 1
),
opp_closed as (
 select close_day as date_day,count(*) filter(where deal_status='Won') as opportunities_won,
 coalesce(round(sum(amount) filter(where deal_status='Won')),0) as opportunities_won_amount,
 count(*) filter(where deal_status='Lost') as opportunities_lost,
 round(sum(amount) filter(where deal_status='Lost')) as opportunities_lost_amount,
 round(sum(amount) filter(where deal_status='Pipeline')) as pipeline_amount
 from opportunity_base where close_day is not null group by 1
)
select d.date_day,lc.leads_created,lcv.leads_converted,t.tasks_completed,e.events_completed,oc.opportunities_created,oc.opportunities_created_amount,
 oh.opportunities_won,oh.opportunities_won_amount,oh.opportunities_lost,oh.opportunities_lost_amount,oh.pipeline_amount
from intermediate.int_salesforce__date_spine d left join lead_created lc using(date_day)
left join lead_converted lcv using(date_day) left join task_daily t using(date_day) left join event_daily e using(date_day)
left join opp_created oc using(date_day) left join opp_closed oh using(date_day)
SQL
cat > sql/marts/salesforce__sales_snapshot.sql <<'SQL'
with context as (
 select current_date as snapshot_date,date_trunc('month',current_date)::date as current_month_start,
 date_trunc('quarter',current_date)::date as current_quarter_start,date_trunc('year',current_date)::date as current_year_start
),
opportunity_metrics as (
 select count(*) as total_opportunities,count(*) filter(where is_won is true) as won_opportunities,
 count(*) filter(where is_closed is true and is_won is not true) as lost_opportunities,count(*) filter(where is_closed is false) as open_opportunities,
 sum(amount) as total_opportunity_value,sum(case when is_won is true then amount else 0 end) as won_amount,
 sum(case when is_closed is true and is_won is not true then amount else 0 end) as lost_amount,
 sum(case when is_closed is false then amount else 0 end) as pipeline_amount,
 avg(days_to_close) filter(where is_closed is true) as avg_days_to_close,avg(amount) filter(where is_won is true) as avg_won_deal_size,
 count(*) filter(where is_created_this_month is true) as opportunities_created_this_month,
 count(*) filter(where is_created_this_quarter is true) as opportunities_created_this_quarter,
 count(*) filter(where is_won is true and is_closed_this_month is true) as opportunities_won_this_month,
 count(*) filter(where is_won is true and is_closed_this_quarter is true) as opportunities_won_this_quarter,
 sum(case when is_created_this_month is true then amount else 0 end) as pipeline_created_this_month,
 sum(case when is_created_this_quarter is true then amount else 0 end) as pipeline_created_this_quarter,
 sum(case when is_won is true and is_closed_this_month is true then amount else 0 end) as revenue_this_month,
 sum(case when is_won is true and is_closed_this_quarter is true then amount else 0 end) as revenue_this_quarter
 from staging.stg_salesforce__opportunity where not coalesce(is_deleted,false)
),
lead_metrics as (
 select count(*) as total_leads,count(*) filter(where is_converted is true) as converted_leads,count(*) filter(where is_converted is false) as open_leads,
 case when count(*)>0 then round(count(*) filter(where is_converted is true)::double/count(*)*100,2) end as conversion_rate_pct
 from staging.stg_salesforce__lead where not coalesce(is_deleted,false)
),
account_metrics as (
 select count(*) as total_accounts,count(distinct owner_id) as unique_account_owners,count(distinct industry) as unique_industries,avg(number_of_employees) as avg_company_size
 from staging.stg_salesforce__account where not coalesce(is_deleted,false)
),
user_metrics as (
 select count(*) as total_users,count(*) filter(where is_active is true) as active_users from staging.stg_salesforce__user
)
select c.*,o.*,l.*,a.*,u.*,
 case when o.total_opportunities>0 then round(o.won_opportunities::double/o.total_opportunities*100,2) else 0.0 end as win_rate_pct,
 case when o.total_opportunities>0 then round((o.won_opportunities+o.lost_opportunities)::double/o.total_opportunities*100,2) else 0.0 end as close_rate_pct
from context c cross join opportunity_metrics o cross join lead_metrics l cross join account_metrics a cross join user_metrics u
SQL

cat > sql/marts/salesforce__revenue_analytics.sql <<'SQL'
with closed_opportunities as (
 select * from intermediate.int_salesforce__opportunity_pipeline where is_closed is true
),
enriched as (
 select op.*,ae.account_size_segment as joined_account_size_segment,ae.geographic_region as joined_geographic_region,
 ae.industry_normalized as joined_industry_normalized,ae.is_high_value_account as joined_high_value,
 u.full_name as owner_name,u.user_type as owner_type,u.performance_tier as owner_performance_tier,u.manager_name as owner_manager
 from closed_opportunities op
 left join intermediate.int_salesforce__account_enhanced ae on op.account_id=ae.account_id
 left join marts.dim_salesforce__user u on op.owner_id=u.user_id
)
select opportunity_id,opportunity_name,account_id,account_name,joined_account_size_segment as account_size_segment,joined_geographic_region as geographic_region,
 joined_industry_normalized as industry_normalized,stage_name,amount,deal_size_category,is_won,
 case when is_won then amount else 0 end as won_revenue,case when is_won is false then amount else 0 end as lost_revenue,
 close_date,year(close_date) as close_year,quarter(close_date) as close_quarter,month(close_date) as close_month,
 fiscal_quarter,fiscal_year,opportunity_age_days as sales_cycle_days,
 case when is_won is true and opportunity_age_days>0 then round(amount/opportunity_age_days,2) else 0.0 end as revenue_per_day,
 case when is_won then case when opportunity_age_days<=30 then 'Fast Win (≤30d)' when opportunity_age_days<=90 then 'Standard Win (31-90d)'
 when opportunity_age_days<=180 then 'Extended Win (91-180d)' else 'Long Cycle Win (>180d)' end
 when is_won is false then case when opportunity_age_days<=30 then 'Fast Loss (≤30d)' when opportunity_age_days<=90 then 'Standard Loss (31-90d)'
 when opportunity_age_days<=180 then 'Extended Loss (91-180d)' else 'Long Cycle Loss (>180d)' end end as revenue_velocity_category,
 quarter(close_date) as seasonal_quarter,
 case quarter(close_date) when 1 then 'Q1 - New Year Start' when 2 then 'Q2 - Mid Year Push' when 3 then 'Q3 - Summer Deals' when 4 then 'Q4 - Year End Close' end as seasonal_period,
 (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(close_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_current_quarter,
 (close_date is not null and ((quarter(current_date)>1 and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(close_date)=quarter(current_date)-1)
 or (quarter(current_date)=1 and year(close_date)=year(current_date)-1 and quarter(close_date)=4))) as is_previous_quarter,
 case when joined_high_value is true and is_won then 'High Value Account Win'
 when joined_high_value is true and is_won is false then 'High Value Account Loss'
 when joined_account_size_segment='Enterprise' and is_won then 'Enterprise Win'
 when joined_account_size_segment='Enterprise' and is_won is false then 'Enterprise Loss' else 'Standard Deal' end as account_value_outcome,
 lead_source,owner_id,owner_name,owner_type,owner_performance_tier,owner_manager,created_date
from enriched
SQL
cat > sql/marts/salesforce__manager_performance.sql <<'SQL'
with manager_rollup as (
 select manager_id,count(distinct owner_id) as total_team_members,
 sum(coalesce(bookings_amount_closed_this_month,0)) as team_bookings_amount_this_month,
 sum(coalesce(bookings_amount_closed_this_quarter,0)) as team_bookings_amount_this_quarter,
 sum(coalesce(total_number_bookings,0)) as team_total_bookings,sum(coalesce(total_bookings_amount,0)) as team_total_bookings_amount,
 sum(coalesce(lost_amount_this_month,0)) as team_lost_amount_this_month,sum(coalesce(lost_amount_this_quarter,0)) as team_lost_amount_this_quarter,
 sum(coalesce(total_number_lost,0)) as team_total_lost,sum(coalesce(total_lost_amount,0)) as team_total_lost_amount,
 sum(coalesce(pipeline_created_amount_this_month,0)) as team_pipeline_created_this_month,
 sum(coalesce(pipeline_created_amount_this_quarter,0)) as team_pipeline_created_this_quarter,
 sum(coalesce(total_number_pipeline,0)) as team_total_pipeline,sum(coalesce(total_pipeline_amount,0)) as team_total_pipeline_amount,
 avg(coalesce(avg_days_to_close,0)) as team_avg_days_to_close,max(coalesce(largest_booking,0)) as team_largest_booking,
 max(coalesce(largest_deal_in_pipeline,0)) as team_largest_pipeline_deal
 from marts.salesforce__owner_performance where manager_id is not null group by manager_id
)
select r.manager_id,r.total_team_members,r.team_bookings_amount_this_month,r.team_bookings_amount_this_quarter,r.team_total_bookings,r.team_total_bookings_amount,
 r.team_lost_amount_this_month,r.team_lost_amount_this_quarter,r.team_total_lost,r.team_total_lost_amount,r.team_pipeline_created_this_month,r.team_pipeline_created_this_quarter,
 r.team_total_pipeline,r.team_total_pipeline_amount,r.team_avg_days_to_close,r.team_largest_booking,r.team_largest_pipeline_deal,
 u.user_name as manager_name,u.email as manager_email,u.title as manager_title,u.department as manager_department,u.city as manager_city,u.state as manager_state,
 case when r.team_total_bookings_amount>0 and r.team_total_lost_amount>0 then round(r.team_total_bookings_amount::double/r.team_total_lost_amount,2) end as win_loss_ratio,
 case when r.total_team_members>0 then round(r.team_total_bookings_amount::double/r.total_team_members,2) else 0.0 end as avg_bookings_per_rep
from manager_rollup r left join staging.stg_salesforce__user u on r.manager_id=u.user_id

cat > sql/marts/salesforce__team_performance.sql <<'SQL'
with revenue_by_owner as (
 select owner_id,sum(won_revenue) as total_revenue_won,
 count(*) filter(where is_won is true) as deals_won,
 avg(sales_cycle_days) filter(where is_won is true) as avg_sales_cycle_days,
 sum(won_revenue) filter(where is_current_quarter) as revenue_current_quarter,
 sum(won_revenue) filter(where is_previous_quarter) as revenue_previous_quarter,
 count(*) filter(where is_won is true and is_current_quarter) as deals_current_quarter
 from marts.salesforce__revenue_analytics group by owner_id
),
member_metrics as (
 select d.user_id,d.full_name,d.manager_id,d.manager_name,d.user_type,d.is_active,d.performance_tier,d.user_tenure_days,d.user_tenure_category,
 p.total_opportunities,p.won_opportunities,p.total_won_amount,p.total_pipeline_amount,p.win_rate_percentage,p.total_leads,p.converted_leads,
 p.total_activities,p.activity_completion_rate_percentage,r.total_revenue_won,r.deals_won,r.avg_sales_cycle_days,r.revenue_current_quarter,r.revenue_previous_quarter,r.deals_current_quarter
 from marts.dim_salesforce__user d
 left join intermediate.int_salesforce__user_performance p on d.user_id=p.user_id
 left join revenue_by_owner r on d.user_id=r.owner_id
 where d.is_active=true
),
team_rollup as (
 select coalesce(manager_id,'No Manager') as team_manager_id,coalesce(manager_name,'No Manager') as team_manager_name,user_type as team_type,
 count(distinct user_id) as team_size,count(distinct user_id) filter(where is_active) as active_team_members,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where performance_tier='High Performer') as high_performers,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where performance_tier='Good Performer') as good_performers,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where performance_tier='Developing') as developing_performers,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where performance_tier='New User') as new_users,
 sum(total_opportunities) as team_total_opportunities,sum(won_opportunities) as team_won_opportunities,
 sum(total_revenue_won) as team_total_revenue,sum(total_pipeline_amount) as team_total_pipeline,
 avg(total_opportunities) as avg_opportunities_per_member,avg(won_opportunities) as avg_wins_per_member,
 round(avg(total_revenue_won)) as avg_revenue_per_member,round(avg(win_rate_percentage),2) as avg_win_rate_per_member,
 sum(total_leads) as team_total_leads,sum(converted_leads) as team_converted_leads,sum(total_activities) as team_total_activities,
 round(avg(activity_completion_rate_percentage),2) as avg_activity_completion_rate,
 sum(revenue_current_quarter) as team_revenue_current_quarter,sum(deals_current_quarter) as team_deals_current_quarter,
 sum(revenue_previous_quarter) as team_revenue_previous_quarter,
 case when sum(team_total_opportunities) over(partition by coalesce(manager_id,'No Manager'),coalesce(manager_name,'No Manager'),user_type)>0 then 0 end as unused_placeholder,
 round(avg(user_tenure_days)) as avg_team_tenure_days,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where user_tenure_category='New User') as new_team_members,
 count(distinct user_id) filter(where user_tenure_category='Veteran') as veteran_team_members
 from member_metrics group by manager_id,manager_name,user_type
),
performer_names as (
 select coalesce(manager_id,'No Manager') as team_manager_id,coalesce(manager_name,'No Manager') as team_manager_name,user_type as team_type,
 arg_max(full_name,total_revenue_won) filter(where total_revenue_won is not null) as top_revenue_performer,
 arg_max(full_name,win_rate_percentage) filter(where win_rate_percentage is not null) as top_win_rate_performer
 from member_metrics group by manager_id,manager_name,user_type
),
scored as (
 select t.*,pn.top_revenue_performer,pn.top_win_rate_performer,
 case when team_total_opportunities>0 then round(team_won_opportunities::double/team_total_opportunities*100,2) else 0.0 end as team_win_rate_percentage,
 case when team_total_leads>0 then round(team_converted_leads::double/team_total_leads*100,2) else 0.0 end as team_lead_conversion_rate,
 case when team_revenue_previous_quarter>0 then round((team_revenue_current_quarter-team_revenue_previous_quarter)::double/team_revenue_previous_quarter*100,2) end as revenue_growth_qoq_percentage,
 case when team_total_activities>0 and team_won_opportunities>0 then round(team_total_activities::double/team_won_opportunities,2) end as team_activities_per_win_ratio
 from team_rollup t left join performer_names pn using(team_manager_id,team_manager_name,team_type)
),
final_scores as (
 select *,least(100.0,greatest(0.0,
 least(team_win_rate_percentage,25)
 + case when team_size>0 and high_performers::double/team_size>=0.3 then 25 when team_size>0 and high_performers::double/team_size>=0.1 then 15 else 5 end
 + case when coalesce(avg_activity_completion_rate,0)>=80 then 20 when coalesce(avg_activity_completion_rate,0)>=60 then 15 else 10 end
 + case when coalesce(revenue_growth_qoq_percentage,0)>10 then 20 when coalesce(revenue_growth_qoq_percentage,0)>0 then 15 when coalesce(revenue_growth_qoq_percentage,0)>=-10 then 10 else 5 end
 + case when coalesce(team_lead_conversion_rate,0)>=20 then 10 when coalesce(team_lead_conversion_rate,0)>=10 then 7 else 3 end))::double as team_health_score
 from scored
)
select team_manager_id,team_manager_name,team_type,team_size,active_team_members,high_performers,good_performers,developing_performers,new_users,
 case when team_win_rate_percentage>=30 and avg_revenue_per_member>=100000 and high_performers>=2 then 'High Performing Team'
 when team_win_rate_percentage>=20 and avg_revenue_per_member>=50000 and high_performers+good_performers>=team_size/2.0 then 'Good Performing Team'
 when team_win_rate_percentage>=10 and avg_revenue_per_member>=25000 then 'Developing Team' else 'Needs Improvement' end as team_performance_tier,
 team_health_score,
 case when team_size>=5 and high_performers>=1 and good_performers>=1 and developing_performers>=1 then 'High Diversity'
 when team_size>=3 and (high_performers>=1 or good_performers>=1) and developing_performers>=1 then 'Medium Diversity'
 when team_size>=2 then 'Low Diversity' else 'Single Member' end as team_diversity_level,
 team_total_opportunities,team_won_opportunities,team_win_rate_percentage,team_total_revenue,team_total_pipeline,round(avg_opportunities_per_member,1) as avg_opportunities_per_member,
 round(avg_wins_per_member,1) as avg_wins_per_member,avg_revenue_per_member,avg_win_rate_per_member,team_total_leads,team_converted_leads,team_lead_conversion_rate,
 team_total_activities,avg_activity_completion_rate,team_revenue_current_quarter,team_deals_current_quarter,revenue_growth_qoq_percentage,team_activities_per_win_ratio,
 top_revenue_performer,top_win_rate_performer,avg_team_tenure_days,new_team_members,veteran_team_members
from final_scores order by team_health_score desc,team_total_revenue desc
SQL

p='sql/staging/stg_salesforce__opportunity.sql';s=open(p).read();s=s.replace('    is_created_this_month as is_created_this_month,','    (created_date is not null and year(created_date)=year(current_date) and month(created_date)=month(current_date)) as is_created_this_month,').replace('    is_created_this_quarter as is_created_this_quarter,','    (created_date is not null and year(created_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(created_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_created_this_quarter,').replace('    days_since_created as days_since_created,','    case when created_date is not null then date_diff(\'day\', created_date::date, current_date) end as days_since_created,').replace('    days_to_close as days_to_close,','    case when close_date is not null and created_date is not null then date_diff(\'day\', created_date::date, close_date::date) end as days_to_close,').replace('    is_closed_this_month as is_closed_this_month,','    (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and month(close_date)=month(current_date)) as is_closed_this_month,').replace('    is_closed_this_quarter as is_closed_this_quarter','    (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(close_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_closed_this_quarter')
open(p,'w').write(s)

select
      id as account_id,
      case when try_cast(amount as DECIMAL(18,2)) >= 0 and try_cast(amount as DECIMAL(18,2)) <= 999999999999.99 then try_cast(amount as DECIMAL(18,2)) else null end as amount,
      campaign_id as campaign_id,
      try_cast(close_date as DATE) as close_date,
      try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) as created_date,
      description as opportunity_description,
      try_cast(expected_revenue as DECIMAL(15,2)) as expected_revenue,
      fiscal as fiscal,
      try_cast(fiscal_quarter as INTEGER) as fiscal_quarter,
      try_cast(fiscal_year as INTEGER) as fiscal_year,
      forecast_category as forecast_category,
      forecast_category_name as forecast_category_name,
      has_open_activity as has_open_activity,
      has_opportunity_line_item as has_opportunity_line_item,
      has_overdue_task as has_overdue_task,
      id as opportunity_id,
      is_closed as is_closed,
      is_deleted as is_deleted,
      is_won as is_won,
      try_cast(last_activity_date as DATE) as last_activity_date,
      try_cast(last_referenced_date as TIMESTAMP) as last_referenced_date,
      try_cast(last_viewed_date as TIMESTAMP) as last_viewed_date,
      lead_source as lead_source,
      name as opportunity_name,
      next_step as next_step,
      owner_id as owner_id,
      case when try_cast(probability as DECIMAL(5,2)) between 0 and 100 then try_cast(probability as DECIMAL(5,2)) else null end as probability,
      record_type_id as record_type_id,
      stage_name as stage_name,
      synced_quote_id as synced_quote_id,
      "type" as type,
      (created_date is not null and year(created_date)=year(current_date) and month(created_date)=month(current_date)) as is_created_this_month,
      (created_date is not null and year(created_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(created_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_created_this_quarter,
      try_cast(days_since_created as INTEGER) as days_since_created,
      try_cast(days_to_close as INTEGER) as days_to_close,
      (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and month(close_date)=month(current_date)) as is_closed_this_month,
      (close_date is not null and year(close_date)=year(current_date) and quarter(close_date)=quarter(current_date)) as is_closed_this_quarter
from raw.opportunity
where id is not null
  and regexp_matches(id, '^[A-Za-z0-9]{15,18}$')
  and not coalesce(is_deleted, false)
  and trim(name) <> ''
  and length(trim(name)) <= 255
select
      id as opportunity_id,
      id as account_id,
      try_cast(amount as DECIMAL) as amount,
      try_cast(close_date as DATE) as close_date,
      try_cast(_fivetran_start as TIMESTAMP) as created_date,
      description as opportunity_description,
      try_cast(expected_revenue as DECIMAL) as expected_revenue,
      forecast_category as forecast_category,
      is_closed as is_closed,
      is_deleted as is_deleted,
      is_won as is_won,
      try_cast(last_activity_date as DATE) as last_activity_date,
      try_cast(last_modified_date as TIMESTAMP) as last_modified_date,
      lead_source as lead_source,
      name as opportunity_name,
      next_step as next_step,
      owner_id as owner_id,
      case when try_cast(probability as DECIMAL) between 0 and 100 then try_cast(probability as DECIMAL) else null end as probability,
      stage_name as stage_name,
      "type" as type,
      md5(concat_ws('|', coalesce(cast(opportunity_id as varchar), ''), coalesce(cast(_fivetran_start as varchar), ''), coalesce(cast(_fivetran_end as varchar), ''))) as history_unique_key
from raw.opportunity_history
where id is not null

import yaml,duckdb
x=yaml.safe_load(open('docs/data_contract.yaml'))['modeling_spec']; c=duckdb.connect('salesforce.duckdb',read_only=True)
for layer,sch in [('intermediate_models','intermediate'),('marts_models','marts')]:
 for m in x[layer]:
  actual=[r[0] for r in c.execute(f'describe {sch}.{m["name"]}').fetchall()]
  expected=[col['name'] for col in m['columns']]
  print(m['name'],'missing',set(expected)-set(actual),'extra',set(actual)-set(expected))
  if expected!=actual: print(' order?',actual)

 p=Path(f'sql/staging/stg_salesforce__{table}.sql');s=p.read_text().replace('status as status,','trim(status) as status,')
 s=s.replace("status in ('Open - Not Contacted', 'Working - Contacted', 'Closed - Converted', 'Closed - Not Converted')", "trim(status) in ('Open - Not Contacted', 'Working - Contacted', 'Closed - Converted', 'Closed - Not Converted')")
 s=s.replace("status in ('Not Started', 'In Progress', 'Completed', 'Waiting on someone else', 'Deferred')", "trim(status) in ('Not Started', 'In Progress', 'Completed', 'Waiting on someone else', 'Deferred')")
 p.write_text(s)
PY

### event 57

## 命令原文

python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
# Correct field source mapping; canonical foreign keys should remain FK columns, not the row's own id.
fixes={
'stg_salesforce__contact':'id as account_id','stg_salesforce__user':'id as account_id,',
'stg_salesforce__opportunity':'id as account_id,','stg_salesforce__opportunity_history':'id as account_id,',
'stg_salesforce__contact_history':'id as account_id,'
}
for table,old in fixes.items():
 p=Path(f'sql/staging/{table}.sql');s=p.read_text()
 if table=='stg_salesforce__contact': s=s.replace(old,'account_id as account_id',1)
 else: s=s.replace(old,old.replace('id as account_id','account_id as account_id'),1)
 if table=='stg_salesforce__user':
  s=s.replace('id as contact_id,','contact_id as contact_id,',1).replace('id as user_role_id,','user_role_id as user_role_id,',1)
 p.write_text(s)
# Contact email and phones: deterministic trims, enforce optional limits by nullifying overlength values.
p=Path('sql/staging/stg_salesforce__contact.sql');s=p.read_text()
for col in ('assistant_phone','home_phone','mobile_phone','other_phone','phone'):
 s=s.replace(f'{col} as {col},',f"case when {col} is null or length(trim({col})) <= 50 then trim({col}) end as {col},")
s=s.replace('photo_url as photo_url,',"case when photo_url is null or length(trim(photo_url)) <= 500 then trim(photo_url) end as photo_url,")
s=s.replace('try_cast(birthdate as DATE) as birthdate,',"case when try_cast(birthdate as date) between date '1900-01-01' and current_date then try_cast(birthdate as date) else null end as birthdate,")
p.write_text(s)
# Opportunity close dates invalid (contract permits only 1900-current+1yr and on/after created date).
p=Path('sql/staging/stg_salesforce__opportunity.sql');s=p.read_text().replace('try_cast(close_date as DATE) as close_date,',"case when try_cast(close_date as date) between date '1900-01-01' and current_date + interval 1 year and (created_date is null or try_cast(close_date as date) >= created_date::date) then try_cast(close_date as date) else null end as close_date,");p.write_text(s)
PY
